Result: 在大规模工具选择基准上的大量实验表明，ToolSearcher在涉及迭代搜索与复杂工具组合的困难设置中，持续优于一系列强基线方法。

Conclusion: 大规模工具选择是智能体强化学习中的一个新挑战，ToolSearcher通过针对性的RL设计有效解决了该问题，验证了细粒度优化与多轮搜索建模在工具选择任务中的价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language processing but struggle to interact with external environments. Tool learning provides a promising way to extend LLMs into actionable agents, where tool selection is a critical prerequisite for successful tool use. Existing work often assumes a small or predefined set of tools, leaving large-scale tool selection underexplored. Real-world repositories contain a vast and diverse array of tools, making it difficult for LLMs to effectively search, distinguish, and compose tools under context-length constraints. We identify large-scale tool selection as a new challenge for agentic reinforcement learning, highlighting that existing RL methods for knowledge-based question answering are inadequate for selecting tools while considering compatibility. To address this challenge, we propose ToolSearcher, a novel RL framework for effective multi-turn search and fine-grained optimization in large-scale tool selection. Specifically, we introduce category-constrained tool discrimination to improve the model's ability to distinguish functionally similar tools, event-level search modeling to explicitly optimize the discovery of target tools during multi-turn search, and trajectory-aligned credit allocation to provide fine-grained reward signals for different stages of the search-selection process. Extensive experiments on large-scale tool selection benchmarks demonstrate that ToolSearcher consistently outperforms a set of strong baselines in challenging settings involving iterative search and complex tool composition.

</details>


### [37] [Training-Free Pronunciation Transcription via Text-Constrained Acoustic Rescoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30924)
*Hikaru Asano,Yotaro Kubo,So Kuroki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的ST2P流水线，在推理阶段融合词汇与声学信息，通过贪心搜索从文本约束候选中选择最优发音，在日语等多语种上大幅降低CER并保持高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TTS训练数据需要准确高效的发音转写。现有方法存在局限：G2P（仅文本）和S2P（仅语音）各只利用部分信息；ST2P虽用两者但依赖昂贵的发音标注数据。因此需要一种无需训练、能同时利用文本与语音信息的方案。

Method: 提出免训练ST2P流水线：推理时集成词汇与声学信息。先用词汇资源和G2P工具生成文本约束的候选发音，再用冻结的预训练S2P模型计算整句负对数似然，通过从左到右的贪心搜索选出最优候选。属于级联（cascade）结构而非直接解码。

Result: 在三个日语语料上，CER从纯文本基线的0.60–1.40%降至0.04–0.17%（参考转写），使用ASR转写为0.64–1.58%；优于所有基线，包括训练过的ST2P模型和商用多模态LLM。贪心搜索在相近CER下比束搜索快3–3.5倍，级联比直接解码快2倍。在西班牙语、法语及初步英语上也超过四个开源多模态LLM和最佳传统方法。

Conclusion: 该免训练级联ST2P方法兼具高准确率与高效率，无需发音标注数据，跨语言泛化良好，为大规模TTS数据准备提供实用方案。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient pronunciation transcription is essential for preparing text-to-speech training data at scale. Existing approaches have different limitations: grapheme-to-pronunciation (G2P) and speech-to-pronunciation (S2P) methods each capture only partial information, using only text or only speech, while speech-and-text-to-pronunciation (ST2P) methods use both but require costly pronunciation-annotated data. To address this problem, we propose a training-free ST2P pipeline that integrates both lexical and acoustic information at inference time. Lexical resources and G2P tools generate text-constrained candidates, and a left-to-right greedy search selects the best one using whole-sequence negative log-likelihoods from frozen pretrained S2P models. On three Japanese corpora, our method reduces Character Error Rate (CER) from 0.60--1.40\% (text-only baseline) to 0.04--0.17\% with reference transcripts, and 0.64--1.58\% with ASR transcripts. It outperforms all baselines, including a trained ST2P model and commercial multimodal LLMs. Our greedy search method is 3--3.5$\times$ faster than beam search at similar CER, and the cascade is 2$\times$ faster than direct decoding ensuring the efficiency and accuracy. In Spanish, French, and preliminary English, it also surpasses four open multimodal LLMs and the best traditional methods.

</details>


### [38] [Estimating and Orthogonalizing Unknown Pre-training Gradients for Continual Fine-tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30935)
*Bing Wang,Changchun Li,Xin-Qiang Cai,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Ximing Li,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EoupCT框架，通过估计并对齐未知的预训练梯度，缓解LLM持续微调中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM持续微调会灾难性遗忘先前任务及通用知识；现有正交梯度投影方法依赖预训练数据与梯度，而这些信息对已发布模型严格未知且高度多样，无法保留预训练通用能力。

Method: 用带Gumbel-Softmax松弛的可学习软提示，动态生成对新手任务最易遗忘的伪数据，从而估计预训练梯度；构建多目标优化问题，引入一阶高效Pareto优化器联合优化LLM参数与软提示，强制新任务更新与估计的预训练梯度正交。

Result: 在多个LLM上的广泛实验表明，EoupCT同时保留任务专精能力与固有通用知识，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: EoupCT为无需访问预训练数据/梯度的持续微调提供可行途径，兼顾新任务学习与通用知识保持。

Abstract: Continual fine-tuning is essential for large language models (LLMs) to dynamically adapt to real-world environments, yet it inevitably suffers from catastrophic forgetting, particularly the performance degradation of previous tasks and LLMs' general-purpose knowledge. Although existing methods, such as orthogonal gradient projection, mitigate the forgetting across various fine-tuning tasks, they fundamentally fail to preserve pre-training LLMs' inherent general-purpose knowledge because the original data and gradients of off-the-shelf pre-training LLMs required by these methods are strictly unknown and highly diverse. To bridge this critical gap, we propose EoupCT, a novel framework designed to Estimate and Orthogonalize Unknown Pre-training gradients for Continual LLM fine-Tuning. Specifically, EoupCT estimates pre-training gradients by dynamically generating pseudo data that is most susceptible to forgetting for new tasks through a learnable soft prompt equipped with Gumbel-Softmax relaxation. Furthermore, we formulate a multi-objective optimization problem and introduce a first-order efficient Pareto optimizer that jointly optimizes LLM parameters and the soft prompt, rigorously enforcing orthogonality between new task updates and the estimated pre-training gradients. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs demonstrate that EoupCT effectively preserves both task-specific proficiency and inherent general-purpose knowledge, successfully mitigating the catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [39] [FAVoR: Measuring and Mitigating Author-Style Homogenization in Federated Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30968)
*Lu Han,Jingyao Zhang,Katy Ilonka Gero,Nguyen H. Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）在多作者个性化写作场景下的“作者风格同质化”问题，并提出FAVoR（联邦作者声音保留）机制，通过共享-私有适配器设计在保持语义续写效用的同时提升作者风格保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为个性化写作助手时，跨多作者适配可能将作者特有信号拉向共享语域，损害个人写作风格。联邦PEFT虽能实现数据本地化的多作者适配，但标准聚合虽能保持续写效用，却使不同作者的生成在风格空间中难以区分，即作者风格同质化。

Method: 作者定义了作者风格同质化这一失效模式，并采用基于角风格分类编码器（ASCE）的诊断方法，在BlogText基准上进行评估，同时使用独立于ASCE的外部作者身份验证。针对同质化问题，提出FAVoR机制，采用共享-私有适配器设计：客户端上传共享适配器更新，同时在本地保留作者特有的残差修正。

Result: 常见联邦PEFT基线在保持语义效用的同时平均化了作者特有信号。在BlogText和外部Mythos-Reddit验证中，FAVoR在作者风格保留方面优于标准和个性化联邦PEFT基线。这些收益伴随较小的续写效用权衡，并得到组件消融、外部验证和冷启动迁移的支持。

Conclusion: standard联邦PEFT聚合会导致作者风格同质化，而FAVoR通过共享-私有适配器与本地残差修正有效缓解该问题，在风格保留上取得优势，代价是轻微的续写效用损失，且结论通过多维度验证具有稳健性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as personalized writing assistants, but adapting a model across many authors can compromise individual writing style by pulling author-specific signals toward a shared register. Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers a data-local setting for this multi-author adaptation problem: clients keep author text local while sharing compact adapter updates. However, we show that standard aggregation can preserve continuation utility while making different authors' generations less distinguishable in style space, a failure mode we define as author-style homogenization. We evaluate author-style retention with Angular Style Classification Encoder (ASCE)-based diagnostics on our main BlogText benchmark and ASCE-independent external authorship verification. Using this protocol, we find that common federated PEFT baselines can preserve semantic utility while averaging out author-specific signals. To address this homogenization, we instantiate FAVoR (Federated Authorial Voice Retention), an author-style residual mechanism for federated PEFT. FAVoR uses a shared-private adapter design: clients upload shared-adapter updates while retaining author-specific residual corrections locally. Across BlogText and external Mythos-Reddit validation, FAVoR improves author-style retention over standard and personalized federated PEFT baselines. These gains come with small continuation-utility trade-offs and are supported by component ablations, external verification, and cold-start transfer.

</details>


### [40] [Coupled Usage-Sense Processes: Temporal and Attributable Lexical Semantic Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30974)
*Haruka Ezoe,Ryohei Hisano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CUSP（Coupled Usage–Sense Processes）框架，通过单一保持边缘分布的时间过程，将词义变化从"变化幅度"扩展为包含时机、机制、成分运动与文本证据的统一分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词汇语义变化研究通常用独立采样各时期分布间的标量距离来概括，只能衡量"变化了多少"，无法揭示"何时变化、由哪些机制与成分运动推动、哪些用法支撑该归因"。

Method: 构建CUSP：以层次耦合通过潜在用法成分关联上下文分布，用马尔可夫组合保证相邻与更长跨度对应关系的一致性；引入位移算子量化变化幅度与时机，将变异精确分解为成分中心移动与成分内部重组，并归因到被输运的成分对；词局部模式刻画不同变化方向及其时间活跃度，代表性段落提供文本依据。在高斯混合特化下给出算子和平方距离的参数可恢复性证明。

Result: 合成实验支持理论预测的收敛速率；在英语和德语DWUG上保持竞争力，并能在保持组合一致性输运的同时恢复受控Janus剖面；大规模美国法院意见语料展示了在无标注自然文本中的转变、模式与段落归因。

Conclusion: CUSP使幅度、时机、机制、运动、模式与文本证据成为同一词汇历史的兼容视角。

Abstract: Lexical semantic change is usually summarized by a scalar distance between independently sampled period distributions. This measures how much a word changed, but does not reveal when it changed, which mechanisms and component movements carried the change, or which usages support the attribution. We introduce Coupled Usage--Sense Processes (CUSP), which derives these answers from a single marginal preserving temporal process. A hierarchical coupling relates contextual distributions through latent usage components, while Markov composition makes adjacent and longer span correspondences compatible. Displacement operators quantify change magnitude and timing, split variation exactly between movement of component centers and reorganization within components, and attribute it to transported component pairs. Word-local modes resolve distinct directions of change and their activity over time, while representative passages from attributed components ground the analysis in text. Under a Gaussian mixture specialization, we prove parametric recovery of the operators and squared distances. Synthetic experiments support the predicted rate. CUSP remains competitive on English and German DWUG and recovers controlled Janus profiles while maintaining compositionally coherent transport. A large corpus of US court opinions demonstrates transition, mode, and passage attribution in unlabeled natural text. CUSP thus makes magnitude, timing, mechanism, movement, modes, and textual evidence compatible views of one lexical history.

</details>


### [41] [THA: Weighted Finite-State Text Normalization and Inverse Text Normalization for Khmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30984)
*Seanghay Yath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tha，一个基于加权有限状态转换器的柬埔寨语文本规范化与逆文本规范化工具包，可一站式完成整行分词、分类与音节边界校验，并在 Google 高棉语测试集上取得高准确率，且以 Apache 2.0 协议开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语在文本转语音与语音识别的双向处理中缺乏维护良好的开源工具；其文字特点（词间无空格、数词嵌入普通词内部）进一步增加了文本规范化与逆文本规范化的难度。

Method: 构建基于加权有限状态转换器（WFST）的工具包 Tha：在一次最短路径搜索中完成整行的分词与分类；另用第二个转换器拒绝高棉语音节内部的词元边界。

Result: 在 Google 高棉语测试集上，274 个基数词全部与参考一致（仅一处拼写变体差异）；在 2,906 条真实 TTS 提示中，其改写的 158 个句子有 153 个正确。

Conclusion: Tha 为高棉语文本规范化与逆文本规范化提供了开源（Apache 2.0）、高准确率的解决方案，有效应对无空格分词与数词嵌套等语言特有难题。

Abstract: Text-to-speech needs written text in spoken form, and speech recognition output needs the reverse. For Khmer, neither direction has a maintained open-source tool, and the script makes both harder: words are not separated by spaces, and number words occur inside ordinary words. We present Tha, a Khmer text normalization and inverse text normalization toolkit built from weighted finite-state transducers. It segments and classifies a whole line in one shortest-path search, and a second transducer rejects token boundaries inside a Khmer syllable. On Google's Khmer test suite, Tha agrees with the reference on all 274 cardinals up to one spelling variant, and on 2,906 real TTS prompts, 153 of the 158 sentences it rewrites are correct. Tha is open source under the Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [42] [Evaluating Sycophancy in Chinese Large Language Models on Factual Questions Derived from Online Search Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30986)
*Geng Liu,Feng Li,Mengxiao Zhu,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过中文事实核查问答，追踪模型在基线、用户信念条件化与反谄媚提示下正确/错误/不确定回答的配对转移，发现反谄媚不等于保持事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益成为信息获取的中介，但其可能表现出谄媚（sycophancy），即迎合用户错误信念，将错误信息呈现为独立验证过的结论，从而强化用户的错误认知。已有研究未厘清：引入用户信念究竟使正确回答变为错误或不确定，还是使不确定回答变为与信念一致的错误回答；也不清楚反谄媚干预是保持/恢复事实准确性，还是仅将回答推向不确定。

Method: 使用中文是非型事实核查问题开展分析。数据涵盖三个中文前沿模型（DeepSeek、Qwen、Doubao）对 12,165 个源自真实中文搜索查询的事实性问题的 364,941 条回答。分别在有无推理、以及基线、信念条件化、反谄媚提示条件下进行评估，追踪正确、错误与不确定回答之间的配对转移，并在错误用户信念下区分“信念一致型错误”与“事实信心丧失”（原本正确的回答转为不确定）。

Result: 谄媚模式随模型与推理设置而异；推理并非一致有效的防护；反谄媚指令可减少错误附和，但往往同时增加不确定性。在错误用户信念下，既可观察到信念一致型错误，也可观察到原本正确答案转为不确定的信心丧失。

Conclusion: 在中文事实问答中，避免附和错误信念并不等同于保持事实准确性，凸显了转移层面（transition-level）评估的价值。此类行为可能通过强化错误信息或削弱用户对正确答案的信心，损害以大模型为中介的信息获取可靠性。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly mediate information access, factually accurate and independent answers are critical. However, these models can exhibit sycophancy by aligning their responses with users' stated beliefs even when those beliefs are incorrect, potentially presenting misinformation as independently verified and reinforcing users' confidence in false claims. Prior work leaves unresolved whether introducing user beliefs causes correct responses to become incorrect or uncertain, or causes uncertain responses to become belief-aligned incorrect answers. It also remains unclear whether anti-sycophancy interventions preserve or restore factual accuracy or merely shift responses toward uncertainty. We analyze factual sycophancy in Chinese-language information seeking using yes/no fact-checking questions. Our analysis covers 364,941 responses from three frontier Chinese-based LLMs (DeepSeek, Qwen, and Doubao) to 12,165 factual questions derived from real-world Chinese search queries. We evaluate the models with and without reasoning across baseline, belief-conditioned, and anti-sycophancy prompting, tracing matched shifts among correct, incorrect, and uncertain responses. Under incorrect user beliefs, we distinguish belief-aligned errors from losses of factual confidence, in which initially correct answers become uncertain. Patterns vary across models and reasoning settings: reasoning is not a consistent safeguard, and anti-sycophancy instructions can reduce incorrect agreement while increasing uncertainty. In Chinese-language factual question answering, avoiding agreement with false beliefs is therefore not equivalent to preserving factual accuracy, highlighting the value of transition-level evaluation. Such behavior may undermine the reliability of LLM-mediated information access by reinforcing misinformation or weakening users' confidence in factually correct answers.

</details>


### [43] [ZooWork-ShopRanker: An Open, Preference-Aligned E-Commerce Reranker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31002)
*Siqiao Xue,Shuxuan Liu,Ning Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向电商场景的重排序器家族 ZooWork-ShopRanker（0.6B/4B/8B），通过对齐 LLM 评委标注的购物偏好标签进行训练，并发布电商偏好基准 ShopRank-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用网页检索训练的开源重排序器难以迁移到电商场景：电商排序不仅取决于主题相关性，还依赖用户偏好、商品约束与比较性匹配，而这些偏好信号难以大规模监督——真实搜索流量虽提供真实查询与候选，却缺乏干净的成对标签。

Method: 以来自不同模型家族的推理大语言模型组成偏好评审团（oracle）标注训练对，采用位置去偏判断与一致性分级；基于标签训练 0.6B、4B、8B 三档重排序器。再以对齐后的 8B 旗舰模型作为蒸馏教师，指导高效的 4B 与 0.6B 模型拟合其分数，并在已评判的成对样本上锐化。同时构建污染受限的基准 ShopRank-Bench（约 10,000 条私有流量偏好对，包含两种文本格式，并按承诺该标签的评委家族数量分级）。

Result: ZooWork-ShopRanker-8B 与 -4B 显著优于最强的开源重排序基线；各模型均显著优于其自身未对齐的基座；ZooWork-ShopRanker-0.6B 优于同规模竞品。增益在两种文本格式下均成立，并延伸至通用 MTEB 基准。

Conclusion: 通过 LLM 评审团偏好对齐与跨规模蒸馏，可有效提升电商重排序性能且兼具高效小模型，相关模型与双格式 ShopRank-Bench 已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Open rerankers trained for general web retrieval transfer imperfectly to e-commerce, where ranking decisions depend not only on topical relevance but also on user preferences, product constraints, and comparative product fit. These preference signals are difficult to supervise at scale: real search traffic provides authentic queries and candidates but no clean pairwise labels. We present ZooWork-ShopRanker, a family of e-commerce rerankers (0.6B, 4B, and 8B) aligned to judge-labeled shopping preference. Training pairs are labeled by a panel of reasoning large language models (LLMs) from different families acting as a preference oracle, with position-debiased judgments and agreement tiers, and the rerankers are trained on these labels. The aligned 8B flagship then serves as a distillation teacher for the efficient 4B and 0.6B models, which are fit to its scores and sharpened on judged pairs. To measure progress, we introduce ShopRank-Bench, a contamination-limited benchmark of ~10,000 private-traffic preference pairs in both text formats, tiered by how many judge families committed to each label. ZooWork-ShopRanker-8B and -4B significantly outperform the strongest open reranker baseline, every model significantly beats its own un-aligned base, and ZooWork-ShopRanker-0.6B beats its size peer; the gains hold in both formats and extend to common MTEB benchmarks. We release the models and the dual-format ShopRank-Bench to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [44] [G$^2$PTQ: Improving LLM Post-Training Quantization with Generalized Gradient Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31009)
*Ruikang Liu,Haoli Bai,Yuxuan Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenzheng Cai,Yanqi Hao,Feiyu Wang,Weidong Zhong,Zhuang Wang,Tong Yang,Xiangsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出G$^2$PTQ，一种结合一阶梯度与二阶Hessian信息的统一训练后量化框架，采用全局监督的块级优化目标，并通过动态刷新梯度/Hessian估计和信赖域缩放机制提升LLM量化效果，超越现有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPTQ类训练后量化方法存在两类互补缺陷：局部逐层目标方法缺乏全局监督；全局目标方法在开始时固定Hessian估计并忽略一阶梯度，导致量化过程中指导信息逐渐过时。

Method: 提出G$^2$PTQ统一框架，在全局监督的块级优化目标下整合一阶与二阶信息；在量化每个Transformer块前刷新梯度与Hessian估计以避免过时；引入信赖域缩放机制动态约束梯度步长以防权重更新爆炸；并推导块级Hessian近似与精确梯度补偿的高效实现。

Result: 在多种模型家族和位宽上的实验表明，G$^2$PTQ与全精度模型的对齐更好，性能超越现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: G$^2$PTQ通过全局监督下融合动态刷新的一阶梯度和二阶Hessian信息，有效解决了现有PTQ方法的监督缺失与指导过时问题，是一种更优的LLM训练后量化方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a practical approach to reducing the memory and computational footprint of large language models (LLMs) without retraining. GPTQ-based methods have become the de facto standard, yet they suffer from two complementary limitations. Methods with local, layer-wise objectives lack global supervision; while methods with global objectives fix their Hessian estimates at the start and ignore first-order gradients, so their guidance grows stale as quantization proceeds. This paper presents G$^2$PTQ, a unified PTQ framework with Generalized Gradient Compensation that integrates both first- and second-order information under a globally supervised, block-wise optimization objective. By refreshing gradient and Hessian estimates before quantizing each Transformer block, G$^2$PTQ avoids the staleness of prior global methods. Furthermore, to stabilize the exact first-order compensation, we introduce a trust-region scaling mechanism that dynamically bounds the gradient step to prevent exploding weight updates. Finally, we derive efficient implementations for block-wise Hessian approximation and exact gradient compensation. Experimental results on various model families and bit-widths demonstrate that G$^2$PTQ enables better alignment with the full-precision model, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/G2PTQ/G2PTQ.

</details>


### [45] [Modeling Student Sensemaking with LLMs and Knowledge-Graph-Guided Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31046)
*Özge Alacam,Zübeyde Demet Kirbulut Güneş,Funda Ekici,Nurcan Turan-Oluk,Dilay Dinçdemir,Hakkı Kadayıfçı,Sevinç Nihal Yeşiloğlu,Burcu Işık,Halil Tümay,Sinem Gencer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估指令微调的中等规模LLM能否在无任务特定训练条件下对协作科学学习中的意义建构进行多维度分析，并检验结构化知识状态信息与推理提示的作用。结果显示：无推理时模型过度预测成功意义建构，启用推理可改善失败案例识别，知识状态诊断进一步提升与专家标注的一致性，但没有任何单一配置在所有维度上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作科学学习需要细致解读学生对话，以刻画学习者如何识别知识缺口、构建解释并推进问题解决；但这类理论驱动的分析劳动密集、难以规模化。作者因此探究指令微调LLM能否在无需任务特定训练的情况下承担这种多维度分析。

Method: 采用两个中等规模指令微调LLM，在23个标注丰富且经专家标注的协作意义建构片段上进行评测。提示条件系统变化，涉及定义性支架、推理模式与对话轮次结构；并引入结构化知识状态信息（知识状态诊断）作为额外依据，与专家标注进行一致性比较。

Result: 在没有推理的情况下，模型倾向于过度预测成功的意义建构；启用推理的提示改善了不成功案例的识别。知识状态诊断提供了额外依据，提高了不成功意义建构的检测效果，并提升了与专家标注的一致性。没有任何单一配置在所有意义建构维度上表现最佳，说明该任务具有多维度特性。

Conclusion: 指令微调LLM在无任务特定训练下可部分支持协作意义建构的多维度分析，结构化知识状态信息与推理提示能缓解过度乐观的预测偏差；但由于不同维度对配置的敏感性不同，实际应用中需按分析维度选择或组合提示策略，而非追求单一通用最优配置。

Abstract: Collaborative science learning requires nuanced interpretation of student dialogue to characterize how learners identify knowledge gaps, build explanations, and work toward resolution - a theory-driven analysis that is labor-intensive and difficult to scale. We investigate whether instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) can support multidimensional analysis of collaborative sensemaking without task-specific training, and whether structured knowledge-state information improves model inference. We evaluate two mid-size LLMs on 23 richly annotated, expert-labeled episodes across prompting conditions that vary definitional scaffolding, reasoning mode, and turn structure. Without reasoning, models tend to overpredict successful sensemaking; reasoning-enabled prompting improves identification of unsuccessful cases. Knowledge-state diagnostics provide additional grounding, improving detection of unsuccessful sensemaking and increasing agreement with expert annotations. No single configuration performs best across all sensemaking dimensions, underscoring the multidimensional nature of the task.

</details>


### [46] [LocUS: Head Selection and Subspace Projection for Targeted Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31122)
*Irene Tallini,Lorenzo Basile,Valentino Maiorca,Francesco Locatello,Alberto Cazzaniga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LocUS方法，通过将激活引导锚定到模型输出词汇子空间，实现精准且低干扰的推理时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准激活引导方法从对比数据中估计每层引导方向，并作用于整层表示空间，可能将干预与对比数据中的非目标属性耦合，从而损害无关能力。

Method: LocUS在反嵌入矩阵中识别属性特定的线性子空间，施加几何约束将引导变换限制在该子空间内，同时把应用范围局部化到稀疏的注意力头子集。

Result: 在三个模型家族上针对毒性缓解、情感转向和谄媚抑制的评估表明，LocUS匹配或超越最先进基线，仅干预不到6%的参数，并更好地保留通用能力。

Conclusion: 将激活引导锚定到输出词汇子空间并结合稀疏注意力头局部化，可在保持通用能力的同时实现有效的模型行为控制。

Abstract: Activation steering is a powerful training-free paradigm for controlling large language models at inference time. However, standard approaches estimate a per-layer steering direction from contrastive data and apply it on the layer's entire representation space, which may couple the intervention to off-target properties present in the contrastive data and degrade unrelated capabilities. To mitigate this issue, we introduce LocUS (Localized Unembedding Steering), a method which grounds activation steering to the model's own output vocabulary subspace. By identifying a property-specific linear subspace within the unembedding matrix, LocUS enforces a geometric constraint that restricts the steering transformation to a specific subspace and at the same time localizes its application to a sparse subset of attention heads. Extensive evaluations across three model families on toxicity mitigation, sentiment redirection and sycophancy suppression show that LocUS matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while intervening on under 6% of parameters and better preserving general capability.

</details>


### [47] [Do we need to answer that question? Salience and Answerability of Potential Questions in Naturalistic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31130)
*Amandine Decker,Maxime Amblard,Ellen Breitholtz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证检验了自然对话中"讨论中的问题"(QUD)建模，通过研究生成式潜在问题的显著性是否能预测其后续被回答，发现二者存在稳健但较弱的正相关，且该效应明显弱于独白文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨QUD建模在自然对话中的适用性，即检验问题的显著性(可及性)是否能预测其随后被实际回答，从而评估该理论框架在会话场景下的解释力。

Method: 基于Wu et al. (2024)，从英国国家语料库(BNC)的话语及其前文语境中自动生成问题，构建包含7,124个问题的数据集，并对其显著性(salience)与可回答性(answerability)进行人工标注，进而分析二者关系，并与独白文本作对比。

Result: 对话中显著性与可回答性之间存在稳健但较低的正相关，即更显著的问题更可能被回应；但该效应明显弱于独白文本。此外，结构化程度高的交互中，标注者间一致性更强，优于组织较松散的对话。

Conclusion: QUD建模可迁移至自然对话，但会话结构比独白文本更难以预测；交互的结构化程度影响显著性判断的一致性与可预测性。

Abstract: We empirically investigate Question Under Discussion based modelling in naturalistic dialogue by studying whether the salience of generated potential questions predicts their subsequent resolution. Building on Wu et al. (2024), we construct a dataset of 7,124 questions automatically generated from utterances and preceding context from the British National Corpus, and annotated for salience and answerability. We find a robust but low positive correlation between salience and answerability in dialogue, indicating that more salient questions are more likely to be addressed. However, this effect is markedly weaker than in monologic text, suggesting that conversational structure is less predictable. We further observe that structured interactions exhibit stronger alignment between annotators than less organised dialogues.

</details>


### [48] [Improving Visual Sensitivity of LLMs on Multimodal Machine Translation with Metric-based Loss Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31169)
*Paweł Mąka,Piotr Andruszkiewicz,Yusuf Can Semerci,Jan Scholtes,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于度量的损失加权（Metric-based Loss Weighting）训练方法，通过提升多模态机器翻译中对图像敏感token的损失权重，增强翻译的视觉 grounding 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态机器翻译虽引入图像等非文本模态以消解歧义，但模型在多模态融合过程中可能忽略图像信息，因此如何提高模型对视觉信息的敏感度仍是待解决的问题。

Method: 利用 Point-wise Cross-mutual Information（PCXMI）指标，比较模型在有/无视觉上下文时输出概率的差异，识别真正受益于图像的token；并进一步提出 Congruency-based PCXMI 指标，将两种指标结合使用，对这些token施加更高的损失权重。

Result: 在三个语言方向的图像引导机器翻译任务上微调三个预训练多模态大语言模型。在 CoMMuTE 对比数据集上，该方法优于所测试的其他方法，相较标准微调准确率提升最高超过7个百分点，同时保持较强的通用翻译性能。

Conclusion: Metric-based Loss Weighting 能有效增强多模态翻译模型的视觉 grounding，在不损害通用翻译能力的前提下显著提升图像相关翻译的准确率。

Abstract: Multimodal Machine Translation aims to incorporate additional signal from non-textual modalities to improve translations by resolving ambiguities. While models, through multimodal fusion, are able to accept images related to the source text, they can ignore this information. Therefore, increasing their visual sensitivity remains an active research area. In this work, we introduce a training method, Metric-based Loss Weighting, that improves visual grounding of translations by increasing the loss function for tokens that benefit from the accompanying image. We identify these tokens using the Point-wise Cross-mutual Information (PCXMI) metric, which compares the model's output probabilities with and without visual context. We introduce a Congruency-based PCXMI metric and experimentally show that both metrics working in combination yield the best results. We evaluate our method by fine-tuning three pretrained Multimodal Large Language Models on the task of Image-guided Machine Translation for three language directions. Metric-based Loss Weighting outperforms other tested methods on the CoMMuTE contrastive dataset, improving accuracy by up to more than 7 percentage points compared to standard fine-tuning, while maintaining strong general translation performance.

</details>


### [49] [Where a Model Sends Its Own Repeated Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31181)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在退化输入（同一token重复）下的argmax映射，将词表映射分为不动点与非常规像两部分：前者作为失败估计量报告，后者（未被记录的部分）用于黑盒模型家族归属，配对源token后家族归属准确率0.8333（随机0.1389），并给出稳健性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作用模型自身token重复的退化输入寻找失效模式，但只关注不动点（模型"停留"处）；作者追问模型"离开"不动点后映射到何处，即补上被前人记为零的、未被记录的映射部分，并将其发展为可用的黑盒模型识别信号。

Method: 对每个token t，一次前向计算argmax p(.|t,t)，得到整个词表上的映射。将其分两半：不动点集合（作为失败估计量，用基数的自然距离、语料操纵分离、家族归属衡量）与非不动点像集合。在配对源token的设计下消除基数混杂，跨7个分词器组与多个语料，对19个模型池中的12个模型做家族归属；设置两个零模型（频率匹配目标、独立边缘），比较家族与分词器预测力，检验循环架构聚类，并测量8位/4位权重量化与语料去重的稳健性边界。所有估计量与终止条件在数据前注册。

Result: 不动点估计量失败：自然距离为83%基数，语料操纵分离仅两位（3471，精度下限为零），家族归属0.5833。非不动点映射在配对后家族归属0.8333（随机0.1389），去掉基数混杂后r从0.9128降到-0.0932；两个零模型分别0.1429与0.0798；家族预测一致性优于分词器（0.2031对0.1205）；循环架构聚类平衡准确率1.0（多数类0.7895），排除主导目标后0.90。稳健性：8位量化影响小于去重语料（0.9004对0.6353），4位破坏映射（0.0098，部署粒度0.1812），精度下限随模型0.201–0.9778变化。

Conclusion: 模型在退化输入下"离开"不动点的映射是稳定、可复现的模型身份信号，配对设计消除基数混杂后家族归属远超随机与两个零模型；该信号对8位量化稳健、对4位量化敏感，且各模型精度下限差异大。作者主张失败的估计量与成功的估计量同样完整报告，并预先注册全部估计量与终止条件。

Abstract: Black-box model identification works by scoring a model's response to natural-language prompts. One line of work feeds models a degenerate input -- their own token, repeated -- to find a failure mode rather than an identity. We take that input and ask where the model goes when it does not. For each token t, read argmax p(. | t, t) in one forward pass; the result is a map on the whole vocabulary, with two halves. The first -- which tokens are fixed points -- is partially anticipated, and we report it as a failed estimand: the natural distance on it is 83% cardinality, separates a corpus manipulation by two bits in 3471 against a precision floor of zero, and attributes families at 0.5833. The second half, where the map sends tokens that are not fixed points, is unrecorded; the one paper holding those tokens logged them as a zero. Pairing on the source token removes the cardinality confound by construction (r from 0.9128 to -0.0932) and attributes families at 0.8333 -- twelve models scored against a pool of nineteen -- with chance 0.1389, across seven tokenizer groups and several corpora. Two nulls clear it: frequency-matched destinations agree at 0.1429, independent marginals at 0.0798. Family predicts agreement better than tokenizer (0.2031 against 0.1205), and recurrent architectures cluster at balanced accuracy 1.0 against a 0.7895 majority rate, or 0.90 once each model's dominant destination is excluded -- the figure we stand behind. We measure the robustness envelope: 8-bit weight rounding moves the map less than deduplicating the training corpus does (0.9004 against 0.6353, on one support), 4-bit destroys it (0.0098; 0.1812 at deployment granularity, so not a coarseness artefact), and the precision floor varies by model from 0.201 to 0.9778. All estimands and kill conditions were registered before the data, and the failed one is reported as fully as the surviving one.

</details>


### [50] [RupeeBias: Auditing Demographic Bias in Indian Economic Guidance from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31245)
*Pavithra P M Nair,Bhavik Talaviya,Shourya Bhushan,Rahul Pankajakshan,Seema Guruvadoo,Avinash Agarwal,Gilad Gressel,Krishnashree Achuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RupeeBias，一个面向印度经济场景的 LLM 人口统计偏见审计基准，涵盖 39,150 条提示、四个用例、六个维度共 87 个印度特有标识，支持英语与 Hinglish，并揭示九个 LLM 在所有维度上的系统性经济建议差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户日益借助大模型获取薪酬、加薪、还价与服务定价等经济建议，而 LLM 已知会复现社会偏见，可能导致用户对自身价值的认知与议价结果被扭曲。现有偏见基准多围绕西方人口类别设计，无法覆盖印度以种姓、城乡等为核心的经济不平等维度，因此需要面向印度语境的经济偏见审计工具。

Method: 构建 RupeeBias 基准：包含 39,150 条提示，覆盖薪资估计、薪资涨幅估计、还价建议、服务定价建议四类用例；采用单属性反事实设计，固定用户资质/经验/服务描述，仅逐次变换一个 demogra phic 标识；覆盖种姓、宗教、地域身份、性别、残障、城乡六轴共 87 个印度特有标识；所有提示均以英语与 Hinglish 双语构建；并用其评测九个 LLM。

Result: 在 RupeeBias 上评测九个 LLM，发现六个维度均存在系统性人口统计差异；对于仅人口标识不同、其余完全相同的提示，LLM 生成的经济输出平均相差 20.2%。

Conclusion: RupeeBias 公开释出，以支持未来针对印度特有社会人口与经济语境下 LLM 经济建议偏见的研究。

Abstract: Individuals turn to large language models (LLMs) for guidance across a wide range of economic tasks, from comparing loan options and planning savings to deciding what raise to ask for or how much to charge for their services. LLMs are known to reproduce social biases, and biased economic guidance may influence what users believe they are worth, what they ask for, and what they ultimately accept. This risk is especially salient in India, where economic outcomes are shaped by demographic categories such as caste and urban-rural location. Existing LLM bias benchmarks, however, are largely designed around Western demographic categories and therefore miss key axes of economic disparity in the Indian context. We introduce RupeeBias, a benchmark for auditing demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across Indian economic settings. RupeeBias consists of 39,150 prompts spanning four use cases: salary estimation, salary increment estimation, counter-offer recommendation, and service pricing recommendation. The benchmark follows a single-attribute counterfactual design, holding the description of the user's qualifications, experience, or service offering fixed while varying one demographic identifier at a time. RupeeBias covers 87 India-specific demographic identifiers across six axes: caste, religion, regional identity, gender, disability, and urban-rural location, with all prompts constructed in both English and Hinglish. We evaluate nine LLMs on RupeeBias and find systematic demographic disparities across all six axes. For otherwise identical prompts that differ only in demographic identifier, LLM-generated economic outputs differ by 20.2% on average. We publicly release RupeeBias to support future research on demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across India-specific demographic and economic contexts.

</details>


### [51] [PIA: A Personal Intelligence Agent Turning Health Conversations into Records and Records into Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31255)
*Jeonghun Yoon,Dongchan Kim,Hongyeon Yu,Young-Bum Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出PIA，一个与消费级健康智能体并行部署的个人智能体，通过四个可控环节（提取、记忆、检索、理解）配以可插拔的健康模块（schema、医学别名词典、知识图谱、时间规则），将对话转化为结构化临床记录并合成用户理解，从而突破通用智能体摘要式记忆在健康场景中的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体记忆依赖摘要、嵌入和top-k检索，适用于一般对话但在健康场景失效：剂量被压缩为一句自然语言、"上周以来"等时间表述交由模型主观消解、三个月的血糖趋势无法靠文本相似度回答。健康智能体无法在摘要之上运行，因此需要一个能自主决定读写方式、并将对话转为类型化临床记录的记忆系统。

Method: PIA接收健康智能体的自然语言请求，自主决定是否以及如何写入或读取，将对话转为类型化临床记录，并将记录合成为对用户的综合理解。其记忆框架由四个控制环节组成——提取、记忆、检索、理解；每个环节都是领域无关机制，并配有可插拔的健康模块：schema、医学别名词典、知识图谱、时间规则。

Result: 同一查询在注入响应上下文的记忆由一维召回、升级为二维健康快照、再升级为带因果关系的三维轨迹时，会得到不同答案。运行经验显示：自报告健康数据为非随机缺失；提问措辞决定合成理解的质量；近三分之一的候选因果链接属于结构性噪声，仅靠规则即可去除。

Conclusion: 健康智能体的记忆不能停留在摘要层面，需以类型化临床记录与多维理解为基础。PIA的领域无关四环节框架加可插拔健康模块，可在从召回、快照到带因果轨迹的递进记忆中支撑同一查询的不同深度回答，并在实际运行中验证了数据缺失机制、提问措辞影响与规则化因果去噪等关键结论。

Abstract: General-purpose agent memory summarizes conversations: it extracts salient snippets, embeds them, and retrieves the top-k into the prompt. A health agent cannot run on summaries: a dose becomes a sentence, "since last week" is resolved at the model's discretion, and a three-month glucose trend cannot be answered by text similarity. We present PIA, a personal intelligence agent deployed alongside a consumer health agent. PIA receives the agent's natural-language requests, decides for itself whether and how to write or read, and turns conversations into typed clinical records and records into a synthesized understanding of the user. Its memory harness consists of four controls -- extraction, memory, retrieval, and understanding -- each a domain-agnostic mechanism with a pluggable health module: schema, medical alias dictionary, knowledge graph, and temporal rules. We show how the same query receives a different answer as the memory injected into the response context deepens from one-dimensional recall, to a two-dimensional health snapshot, to a three-dimensional trajectory with causality, and report lessons from operation: self-reported health data are missing not at random, question phrasing governs the quality of synthesized understanding, and nearly a third of candidate causal links are structural noise that rules alone remove.

</details>


### [52] [MoSAR: Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes for Learning Adaptive and Approximable Attention Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31261)
*Michele Paolicelli,Alessandro Petruzzelli,Alessandro Franceso Maria Martina,Cataldo Musto,Giovanni Semeraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出MoSAR（Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes），将注意力近似视为几何问题，通过输入条件路由学习短/中/全局三种注意力机制的混合，形成连续的距离依赖注意力场，在保持语言建模质量的同时学习更低覆盖范围的注意力几何，并在长度外推任务上超越ALiBi等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自注意力的二次复杂度是长上下文语言建模的核心瓶颈。现有高效方法大多预先决定注意力稀疏或局部的位置，但自然语言依赖关系是输入相关的，难以事先规定。作者主张应将注意力近似作为几何问题处理，让模型从数据中学习交互几何，即学习位置相关性在哪衰减、在哪需保留更广交互。

Method: 提出MoSAR，学习一种自适应、可控衰减的查询-键交互几何。在位置编码之后应用输入条件的查询和键路由器，选择短、中、全局三种注意力机制的混合，诱导出连续的距离依赖注意力场而非固定稀疏模式。该几何在训练中学习，之后可通过top-1路由进行离散化。

Result: 在匹配500M参数模型的受控预训练实验中，MoSAR学到覆盖范围显著更低的注意力几何，且在训练上下文长度上的困惑度优于密集RoPE，语言建模质量不下降。在长度外推下，MoSAR在所有评估变体中取得最佳困惑度，包括ALiBi等强基线。学习到的几何在确定性top-1离散化下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 学习到的注意力几何既具自适应性，又适合推理时的低成本近似，说明将注意力近似作为可学习几何问题是有前景的方向。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of dense self-attention remains a central bottleneck for long-context language modeling. Many efficient alternatives address this cost by deciding in advance where attention should be sparse or local. We argue that attention approximation should instead be approached as a geometric problem, with the relevant interaction geometry learned from data: natural-language dependencies are input-dependent and difficult to prescribe in advance, so the model should learn where positional relevance can decay and where broader interactions must be preserved. We introduce Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes (MoSAR), which learns such an adaptive, controlled-decay geometry over query--key interactions. Input-conditioned query and key routers, applied after positional encoding, select mixtures over short, medium, and global regimes, inducing a continuous distance-dependent attention field rather than a fixed sparsity pattern. This geometry is learned during training and can subsequently be discretized through top-1 routing. In controlled pre-training experiments with matched 500M-parameter models, MoSAR learns a substantially lower-reach attention geometry without degrading language-modeling quality, improving perplexity over dense RoPE at the training context length. Under length extrapolation, MoSAR achieves the best perplexity among all evaluated variants, including strong baselines such as ALiBi. Moreover, the learned geometry remains stable under deterministic top-1 discretization, suggesting that it is not only adaptive, but also amenable to low-cost approximation at inference time.

</details>


### [53] [Identifying Scientists on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31264)
*Philipp Meier,Katarina Boland,Laura Kallmeyer,Stefan Dietze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于X/Twitter用户简介和推文自动识别科学家与非科学家账号的方法，在两组数据集上，随机森林用语言学特征达F1约0.88，对比微调DeBERTa集成达约0.96，并公开了两个带标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络上科学相关话语日益重要，经典知识秩序受到侵蚀，因此需要自动识别科学家等不同用户群体。

Method: 基于X/Twitter用户的个人简介（biographies）与推文进行账号分类；采用两类模型路线：一是使用语言学特征结合随机森林；二是使用对比式微调（contrastively fine-tuned）的DeBERTa，并构建集成模型。

Result: 在两个不同数据集上均能区分科学家与非科学家账号；随机森林结合语言学特征F1最高约0.88；对比微调DeBERTa在集成设置下F1最高约0.96；同时提供了两个标注数据集，包含X用户及其对应推文与用户简介。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效自动识别科学家与非科学家账号，且高精度可由预训练语言模型集成实现，相关数据集为后续研究提供支撑。

Abstract: With the growing importance of science-related discourse on the Web and the erosion of the classical knowledge order, it is important to identify different user groups, such as scientists, automatically. This work proposes an approach for identifying scientists and non- scientists on X/Twitter based on their user biographies and tweets. We show that we are able to classify accounts as scientists and non- scientists on two different datasets, reaching an F1 score of up to 0.88 using Random Forests with linguistic features and up to 0.96 using a contrastively fine-tuned DeBERTa model in an ensemble setup. Furthermore, we provide two datasets with X users labeled as scientists or non scientists and their respective tweets and user biographies.

</details>


### [54] [Stale-Document Poisoning: When Outdated Retrieval Overrides Correct Model Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31342)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文识别出RAG中的"过期文档投毒"时效对齐失败问题，构建317个知识反转基准，发现过期证据会大幅翻转模型答案，且需显式的有效性信息才能纠正，并提出时效感知混合重排序缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）常被用于提供外部证据以弥补知识过时问题，但检索仅在证据仍然有效时才有帮助。作者试图揭示并量化一种被忽视的时效对齐失败：即检索到的过期证据会使本可正确回答的模型出错，从而强调模型需要具备"选择性信任"能力——不仅判断证据说了什么，还要判断其是否仍然适用。

Method: 1) 构建包含317个经验证的知识反转（knowledge reversals）基准，涵盖医学、法律、软件和平台政策四个领域，均基于带日期的官方来源；2) 在12个模型上评估，进行检索投毒实验（含无指令与显式遵循指令两种条件）；3) 在50个反转上保持历史证据不变，仅改变评估日期，以隔离时效适用性；4) 通过因果干预验证有效性信息对最终决策的作用；5) 提出固定时效感知的混合重排序器（recency-aware hybrid re-ranker）。

Result: 近期医学反转比长期确立的更困难。过期检索在无信任指令下翻转30%的Llama和37%的Qwen答案，显式遵循指令下升至66%和75%。在4个开源模型和4个领域中投毒率为17-91%，而匹配的最新证据在97-100%的试验中被遵循。仅靠日期只能带来有限适应，但显式告知旧证据失效时点后，较大模型几乎完美切换到正确答案。因果干预证实有效性信息直接塑造最终决策，且相同内部组件支持更广泛的比较任务。固定时效感知混合重排序在日期准确时减少4.6-10.0个百分点的投毒，其增益依赖可靠的时间元数据。

Conclusion: RAG的可靠性要求选择性信任：模型不仅要判断检索证据的内容，还要判断其是否仍然适用。时间适用性可能调用一种通用的比较推理机制，而缓解过期文档投毒需依赖准确的时效元数据和显式的有效性信息。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often used to address outdated knowledge by providing external evidence. But retrieval helps only when that evidence is still valid. We identify a temporal alignment failure, stale-document poisoning, in which outdated evidence makes a model wrong despite answering correctly without retrieval. We construct a benchmark of 317 verified knowledge reversals across medicine, law, software, and platform policy, grounded in dated official sources. Across 12 models, recent medical reversals are harder than long-established ones. More importantly, outdated retrieval flips 30% of Llama and 37% of Qwen answers even without instructions to trust the document; explicit follow instructions raise these rates to 66% and 75%. Across four open models and four domains, poisoning ranges from 17-91%, while matched up-to-date evidence is followed in 97-100% of trials. To isolate temporal applicability, we keep the historical evidence unchanged across 50 reversals and vary only the evaluation date. A clear pattern emerges: dates alone produce only modest adaptation, but when models are explicitly told when the old evidence stops applying, the larger models switch to the appropriate answer almost perfectly. Causal interventions confirm that this validity information directly shapes the final decision. The same internal components also support broader comparison tasks, suggesting that temporal applicability can recruit a general reasoning mechanism used for other comparisons. Finally, a fixed recency-aware hybrid re-ranker reduces poisoning by 4.6-10.0 points when dates are accurate, with gains that depend on reliable temporal metadata. Reliable RAG therefore requires selective trust: models must determine not only what retrieved evidence says, but whether it still applies.

</details>


### [55] [Highlight-Then-Summarize: Learning to Compress Evidence for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31382)
*Zhaoyuan Xia,Qinghongbing Xie,Yung Xiang Hue,Jianguang Jiang,Gaofeng Lu,Zhenyu Jiao,Xing Yuan,Dai Dai,Tong Mo,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Highlight-Then-Summarize（H2S），一种"先压缩再推理"的长上下文处理范式：先定位与问题相关的源证据，再整合为紧凑的问题条件摘要，最后生成答案。配套构建了H2S-Dataset数据集与H2S-RL过程级奖励训练方法。H2S-14B在H2S-Bench上平均得分32.60，超过Qwen3.8-27B达10.17分，并在4K输出预算下保留97.1%的16K预算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文理解要求LLM在冗长文档、对话和代码上进行推理，但任务相关证据往往稀疏且分散在大量无关冗余内容中，直接推理效率低、性能受限。因此需要一种显式筛选并整合证据的机制，使推理更聚焦、生成更紧凑。

Method: 提出compress-then-reason范式H2S：第一步识别有原文支撑、与问题相关的证据；第二步将其整合为紧凑的、以问题为条件的摘要；第三步基于摘要生成最终答案。为训练该行为，构建H2S-Dataset（6,647条样本，覆盖11个基准家族，平均上下文长度43.9K tokens），并提出H2S-RL，在最终答案正确性之外，为证据选择与摘要构建提供过程级奖励。

Result: 在七任务长上下文套件H2S-Bench上，统一采用128K输入与4K输出预算，H2S-14B平均得分32.60，较Qwen3.8-27B高10.17分，在所评估的开源模型中取得最佳整体结果；同时获得最高Evidence-Summary Quality分数，并在4K输出预算下保留其16K预算性能的97.1%。

Conclusion: 显式地选择并整合证据能够提升长上下文推理能力，同时支持更紧凑的生成，说明"先压缩再推理"是应对稀疏证据长上下文任务的有效路径。

Abstract: Long-context understanding requires large language models (LLMs) to reason over lengthy documents, conversations, and code, yet task-relevant evidence is often sparse and scattered amid substantial irrelevant and redundant content. We propose Highlight-Then-Summarize (H2S), a compress-then-reason paradigm that first identifies source-grounded, question-relevant evidence and then integrates it into a compact, question-conditioned summary before producing the final answer. To train this behavior, we construct H2S-Dataset, comprising 6,647 examples from 11 benchmark families with an average context length of 43.9K tokens, and introduce H2S-RL, which provides process-level rewards for evidence selection and summary construction in addition to final-answer correctness. We evaluate on H2S-Bench, a seven-task long-context suite. Under a shared 128K input and 4K output budget, H2S-14B achieves an average score of 32.60, outperforming Qwen3.8-27B by 10.17 points and obtaining the strongest overall result among the evaluated open-source models. H2S-14B also achieves the highest Evidence-Summary Quality score and retains 97.1% of its 16K-budget performance with only a 4K output budget. These results show that explicitly selecting and integrating evidence improves long-context reasoning while enabling more compact generation.

</details>


### [56] [Sorry Robot, Happy Human: Vision-Language Models Read Only One of Two Legible Typographic Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31403)
*Mert İncidelen,Yamen Kashkash,Asya Berker,Murat Aydoğan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究构建 DecoyBench 数据集（300 张图，含叠加的轮廓文字与阴影文字），在两种提示、两种分辨率下评测 6 个闭源 VLM；模型在高分辨率仅能读取轮廓层文字，在低分辨率仅能读取阴影层文字，与人类可同时读取两层的表现不一致，揭示其在多空间频率文字层处理上的系统性行为局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）虽在 OCR 任务上表现良好，但易受排版攻击（typographic attacks），且对包含多层文字的图像结构处理脆弱。研究旨在系统刻画这种脆弱性，特别是模型在多层空间频率文字结构中的读取能力边界。

Method: 提出 Decoy Font 方法并构建 DecoyBench 数据集，共 300 张图像，每张由锐利轮廓线文字叠加于柔和阴影文字之上构成。选取 3 个模型家族的 6 个近期闭源模型，在两种提示条件（naive 与 guided）和两种分辨率（512×512 与 64×64）下进行评测；同时开展人类被试验证研究作为对照基准。

Result: 人类被试能高准确率读取两层文字。模型方面：高分辨率下，多数变体与两种提示方法均能近人类水平读取轮廓文字，但几乎完全无法提取阴影文字；低分辨率下，轮廓文字对模型与人类均不可读，而阴影文字可被高准确率提取。

Conclusion: 所评测的 VLM 在处理含多个空间频率层的排版结构时表现出与人类不同的、一致性的行为局限：无法在同一条件下同时解析轮廓层与阴影层文字。该发现表明当前 VLM 的 OCR 能力对文字的空间频率特性高度敏感，存在系统性盲区。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), despite their success in optical character recognition (OCR) tasks, are vulnerable to typographic attacks and have a fragile structure for images with multiple text layers. In this study, the DecoyBench dataset was created using the Decoy Font method. The dataset consists of 300 images, each containing text with sharp contour lines superimposed on another text with soft shading. Six recent closed-source models from three different model families were evaluated using this dataset under two different prompting conditions (naive and guided) and at two different resolutions ($512\times512$ and $64\times64$). A validation study showed that human participants could read both text layers with high accuracy. In contrast, the models, with most variants and both prompting methods, read the contour text with near-human accuracy at high resolution, but almost never fully extracted the shading text. At low resolution, the contour text could not be read by either the models or humans, while the shading text could be extracted with high accuracy. The findings indicate that the evaluated VLMs exhibit a consistent behavioral limitation when processing typographic structures containing multiple spatial frequency layers.

</details>


### [57] [ViSTA: A Simple Bridge Extends Visual Alignment to Clinical Time-Series Understanding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31448)
*Junyi Gao,Yu Shi,Pingzhao Hu,Ewen M Harrison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ViSTA适配器，将不规则数值测量注入预训练视觉-语言模型的图表表示中，仅训练极少参数即可实现临床风险预测与时序问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型依赖结构化高维时序数据做风险估计，而大语言模型擅长医学文本理解与问答；但语言能力并不能保证对结构化临床时序的准确预测。将二者能力打通，可把风险估计与关于患者病情演变的灵活提问联系起来。

Method: ViSTA是一个紧凑适配器，将不规则的数值测量整合进预训练视觉-语言模型的图表（chart）表示中。它对视觉token学习修正量，同时保持所有预训练参数不变（仅约0.516M可训练参数）。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV上，针对急性肾损伤和死亡预测，ViSTA在2–9B参数各模型上四项指标均取得最高平均分。2B模型以0.516M可训练参数达到AKI预测AUROC 0.7376，接近GPT-5.6 Sol文本输入+高推理强度的0.7380。时序问答训练在4B参数下达69.27%准确率，可训练参数比使用图表或数值文本的LoRA少90%以上，准确率差距2.82–4.88个百分点。

Conclusion: ViSTA将预训练语言模型扩展到数值预测与时序问答任务，展示了以极小训练代价连接语言模型与临床时序建模的可行性。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models estimate risk from patient measurements, while large language models support medical text understanding and question answering. Yet their language capabilities do not ensure accurate prediction from structured, high-dimensional clinical time series. Improving this ability would connect risk estimation with flexible questions about a patient's evolving condition. We introduce ViSTA, a compact adapter that incorporates irregular numerical measurements into a pretrained vision-language model's chart representations. It learns corrections to visual tokens while leaving all pretrained parameters unchanged. On MIMIC-IV, ViSTA has the highest mean scores among the compared adaptations on all four metrics for acute kidney injury and mortality prediction across models with 2-9 billion parameters. With 0.516 million trainable parameters, the 2-billion-parameter model reaches an area under the ROC curve of 0.7376 for acute kidney injury, compared with GPT-5.6 Sol's 0.7380 with text input and high reasoning effort. Training for temporal question answering yields 69.27% accuracy at 4 billion parameters with over 90% fewer trainable parameters than low-rank adaptation using charts or numerical text, at a 2.82-4.88 percentage-point accuracy gap. ViSTA extends pretrained language models to numerical prediction and temporal questions.

</details>


### [58] [Evaluating Cultural Awareness of LLMs for Haitian Creole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31506)
*Christelle Clervilsson,Yanzhu Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估大语言模型在海地克里奥尔语上的文化意识，从特异性、偏见、多样性、变异性四个维度进行基准测试，揭示其与高资源法语之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高资源与低资源语言间存在显著性能差距，且常无法捕捉欠代表社群的文化规范与价值观。海地克里奥尔语使用者众多但数字资源严重匮乏，其文化意识评估此前尚属空白。

Method: 由母语者策划文化显著性提示，构建基准，在文本填空设置下沿特异性、偏见、多样性、变异性四个互补维度评估LLM的文化意识，并与高资源法语进行对比，辅以故事生成分析。

Result: 海地克里奥尔语与法语之间存在明显的文化意识差距；海地语表现跨领域更不均衡，且更受法语语言干扰。故事生成中反复出现以苦难与韧性刻画海地角色的模式，表明即便是正面刻画也可能编码刻板叙事。

Conclusion: 低资源语言的文化意识评估需多维度考量，正面表征亦可能隐含刻板印象；作者公开代码、基准与评估框架以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit substantial performance disparities between high- and low-resource languages. Beyond lower task performance, they often fail to capture the cultural norms and values of underrepresented communities. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of cultural awareness in LLMs for Haitian Creole, a language spoken by millions but severely underrepresented in digital resources. We assess cultural awareness along four complementary dimensions---specificity, bias, diversity, and variation---using a benchmark of culturally salient prompts curated by native speakers in a text infilling setting. Our results reveal a clear gap between cultural awareness in Haitian Creole and higher-resource French, with Haitian performance being more uneven across domains and more affected by French linguistic interference. Story generation further reveals recurring portrayals of Haitian characters through hardship and resilience, showing that even positive characterizations can encode stereotypical narratives. Our code, benchmark, and evaluation framework are publicly available.

</details>


### [59] [Muslim: A Deployed Arabic Voice AI Platform for Grounded Islamic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31511)
*Yahya Mohamed Elnawasany*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Muslim——一个面向真实用户、提供有据可依的伊斯兰知识的生产级阿拉伯语语音 AI 平台，除实时语音管线与确定性多源检索层外，还发布了微调模型工件、账户计量层与三层可观测性栈，并给出实测延迟与准确率数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究原型通常缺少可发布模型、运营化账户体系与针对特定故障模式的可观测性；本文将开放演示转化为可运维、抗滥用的生产产品，以弥合这一差距。

Method: 系统由实时语音管线（NeMo 阿拉伯语 ASR、OpenAI 兼容 LLM 端点、自托管 TTS）与跨六个 MCP 服务器路由的确定性多源检索层构成；发布工具路由 LLM（Muslim-6B-PRO，5.94B 参数）与 MSA TTS 模型（Fasih-TTS-V1）；设计按账户免费轮次额度、容量感知拒答与延迟邮箱验证的计量层；并搭建针对 GPU 绑定代理主机静默、而 Web 层照常服务这一故障模式的三层可观测性栈（存活、错误上报、产品分析）。

Result: 实测显示：诵经校验准确率 98.4%（124 例）；端到端语音延迟 0.9–1.7 秒；Fasih-TTS-V1 在社区投票的阿拉伯语 TTS Arena（MSA）上总分 17 个系统第 5、11 个开放权重系统中第 2。

Conclusion: 论文报告了在生产环境运行伊斯兰知识语音产品的具体工程权衡与局限，表明研究原型可通过模型发布、计量层与面向特定故障模式的可观测性补齐运营化能力。

Abstract: We present Muslim, a production Arabic voice AI platform serving grounded, sourced Islamic knowledge to real users. Beyond a real-time voice pipeline (NeMo Arabic ASR, an OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoint, self-hosted TTS) and a deterministic multi-source retrieval layer routed across six Model Context Protocol servers, we report three things a research prototype typically lacks. First, a released family of fine-tuned Arabic Islamic model artifacts: an efficient tool-routing LLM (Muslim-6B-PRO, 5.94B parameters) and a Modern Standard Arabic TTS model (Fasih-TTS-V1) that ranks 5th of 17 overall and 2nd of 11 open-weight systems on the community-voted Arabic TTS Arena for MSA. Second, an account and metering layer - a free per-account turn allowance, capacity-aware refusal, and email verification deferred to the point it actually matters - that turns an open demo into an operable, abuse-resistant product. Third, a three-layer observability stack (liveness, error reporting, product analytics) built specifically around the system's characteristic failure mode: a GPU-bound agent host going silent while the web tier keeps serving normally. We report real, measured latency and accuracy figures (98.4% recitation-validation accuracy on 124 cases; end-to-end voice latency of 0.9-1.7s) and discuss the concrete engineering trade-offs and limitations of running an Islamic-knowledge voice product in production.

</details>


### [60] [MexHat: A Dataset for Hate Speech Detection in Mexican Spanish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31553)
*Itzel Tlelo-Coyotecatl,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MexHat——一个面向墨西哥西班牙语语境的视频仇恨言论检测数据集，包含约1k个视频片段，标注了三分支分类及细粒度仇恨言论子类别，并给出了统计与基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线内容监控中，仇恨言论检测至关重要，但该任务本质上依赖语境线索以准确理解内容意图。当前自动化方法虽有进展，非英语资源仍严重匮乏，限制了模型对多模态内容中微妙、依赖语境且与文化相关特征的适应能力。因此，作者希望填补墨西哥西班牙语语境下的资源空白。

Method: 作者构建了MexHat视频数据集，聚焦墨西哥西班牙语的语言与文化线索。数据集约含1k个视频片段，并设计了两类标注任务：1）三分类评估（无负面内容、冒犯性内容、仇恨言论内容）；2）细粒度评估，包含三个仇恨言论子类别。随后基于该数据集开展统计分析与基线实验。

Result: 数据集统计与基线结果表明该任务本身具有内在挑战性。论文同时声明内容涉及敏感信息，可能引起部分读者不适。

Conclusion: MexHat为墨西哥西班牙语多模态仇恨言论检测提供了新的资源，支持三分类与细粒度子类别标注，并揭示了任务难度。该资源有助于缓解非英语语境下资源稀缺问题，推动面向文化与语境敏感内容的多模态检测研究。

Abstract: Ensuring online safety through content monitoring had raised Hate Speech Detection as a crucial task to be addressed. By essence the task demands the capture of contextual cues, which are essential for a precise understanding of the content's intent. Although automated detection approaches for the task have advanced significantly, the scarcity of non-English resources persists, limiting the ability of models to adapt to the subtle, context-dependent, and culturally related nature of multimodal content. In this paper, we introduce MexHat, a video dataset designed to capture the linguistic and cultural cues for the hate-speech detection task in a Mexican Spanish context. Our dataset comprises around 1k video clips annotated across two tasks: a three-way class evaluation (no negative content, offensive content and hate-speech content), and a fine-grained class evaluation including three hate-speech sub-categories. The dataset statistics and the baseline results highlight the inherent challenges associated with the task. Disclaimer: This paper contains sensitive content that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [61] [Strategically Diverse Sampling for Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31571)
*Alexander Gurung,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出以"策略多样性"（strategic diversity）替代传统IID采样+正确性过滤来构建自训练数据，并给出GROOT与Verbalized Sampling两种采样方法；在竞赛编程与Next-Chapter Prediction任务上，策略多样但错误的样本甚至能超越235B教师模型的IID蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练与推理方法（如RL、test-time scaling）依赖重复采样，但只有响应存在实质差异时才有收益。自训练的数据构建通常采用IID采样并以正确性为主要过滤标准，导致模型自身偏好的策略被过度代表，缺乏策略层面的多样性。作者因此探究"策略多样性"作为自训练数据构建的新原则。

Method: 提出并采用两种策略多样化采样方法：GROOT——构建层次化的方法树并采样不同路径；Verbalized Sampling（VS）——经改造后生成无结构的方法集合。以此构建策略多样化的自训练数据，并与IID采样数据进行对比实验。

Result: 在competitive programming与Next-Chapter Prediction两个领域，策略采样训练的模型在困难任务上优于IID训练模型，且为RL和test-time scaling提供了更强的初始化。最显著的结果是：使用Qwen3-4B生成的策略多样但答案错误的轨迹进行自训练，效果超过从235B教师模型进行IID蒸馏。

Conclusion: 结果挑战了关于自训练数据价值的主流假设，表明方法/策略的多样性可能比正确性和教师规模更重要。

Abstract: Many LLM training and inference methods, including RL and test-time scaling, depend on repeated sampling, but benefit only when the responses meaningfully differ. Self-training faces the same challenge: training data is typically constructed by sampling IID responses and filtering primarily for correctness, thereby overrepresenting strategies a model already favours. We investigate strategic diversity, or substantive variation among approaches to a problem, as an alternative principle for constructing self-training data. We generate strategically diverse data with two sampling methods: GROOT, a new method which constructs a hierarchical tree of approaches and samples distinct paths, and Verbalized Sampling (VS), adapted to produce an unstructured set of approaches. Across competitive programming and Next-Chapter Prediction domains, models trained on strategically sampled data outperform IID-trained counterparts on difficult tasks and provide strong initializations for RL and test-time scaling. Most strikingly, self-training on strategically diverse but incorrect traces from Qwen3-4B outperforms IID distillation from a 235B teacher. These results challenge prevailing assumptions about what makes useful self-training data and show that diversity of approaches can matter more than correctness or teacher scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [Nearest but Not Dearest: Shared Curator-Feedback Infrastructure for Content-Only Search and Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30568)
*Matt Sandler*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在仅内容信号的B2B音乐发现平台上，将策展人拒绝划分为"声音失败"（编码器可处理）与"上下文失败"（与波形正交），并分别路由至候选生成约束过滤器与表征层嵌入重加权头，使拒绝率由38.17%降至28.83%（相对下降24.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的B2B音乐发现平台同时提供查询驱动检索（文本提示、氛围标签）与种子驱动推荐（种子曲目、艺人电台），二者共享同一授权曲库、同一LAION-CLAP音文联合嵌入空间、同一候选生成过滤器与同一排序头，且均不消费终端听众行为信号。在仅内容（content-only）范式下，策展人判断是主要反馈信号，但离线余弦相似度对其预测效果很差：余弦最近邻中有38%被策展人拒绝。因此需要一种能利用策展反馈、且能同时服务两种范式的反馈基础设施。

Method: 对拒绝样本进行声音-上下文二分分解：55%为编码器可处理的"声音失败"（风格、速度、情绪不匹配），37%为与波形正交的"上下文失败"（语言、节日内容、信仰类内容、版权与歌词标记）。将该分解作为反馈基础设施，按失效模式路由到可吸收该信号的层：上下文失败路由至候选生成阶段的约束过滤器，声音失败路由至表征层的嵌入重加权头；二者均位于搜索/推荐分叉之下，因此单一策展人回路可同时维护两种体验。在相隔一个月的两个生产轮次中收集1,200条策展判断进行评估，采用McNemar卡方检验，并做归因分解。

Result: 综合干预将拒绝率从38.17%降至28.83%（相对下降24.5%，McNemar卡方=22.4，p=2.2e-6）。归因分解显示：4.08个百分点来自过滤器可处理的类别，5.25个百分点来自其余类别。该部署为无盲、复合干预，故该结果为生产环境下的归因上界（accounting bound），而非因果估计。

Conclusion: 本文将工作定位为工业案例研究而非经验证的通用方法。其面向仅内容发现的经验启示为：失效划分（声音 vs 上下文）与范式划分（搜索 vs 推荐）相互正交，而修正落在两种范式共享的层上，因此单一策展反馈回路即可同时改进检索与推荐。

Abstract: A deployed B2B music-discovery platform serves both query-driven search (text prompts, vibe tags) and seed-driven recommendation (seed-track and artist stations) over one licensed catalog, one LAION-CLAP joint audio-text embedding space, one candidate-generation filter, and one ranking head -- and neither path consumes end-listener behavioral signal. In this content-only regime, curator judgment is the principal feedback signal available, and offline cosine similarity predicts it poorly: 38% of cosine-nearest neighbors are rejected by curators. The rejections reveal a clean partition: a majority (55%) are sound failures the encoder could address (style, tempo, mood mismatch), and a substantial minority (37%) are context failures orthogonal to the waveform (wrong language, holiday content, devotional content, rights and lyric flags). We deploy this sound-vs-context decomposition as feedback infrastructure, routing each failure mode to the layer that can absorb it: context failures to a constraint filter at candidate generation, sound failures to an embedding reweighting head at the representation layer -- both below the search/recommendation split, so a single curator loop maintains both experiences. On 1,200 curator judgments collected over two production rounds one month apart, the combined intervention reduces rejection rate from 38.17% to 28.83% (-24.5% relative, McNemar chi-squared = 22.4, p = 2.2e-6). An accounting decomposition attributes 4.08 pp of the drop to filter-eligible categories and 5.25 pp to the rest; the deployment was unblinded and compound, so this is a production accounting bound, not a causal estimate. We present this as an industrial case study rather than a validated general method, and close with lessons for content-only discovery: the failure partition is orthogonal to the paradigm partition, and corrections land in layers shared by both paradigms.

</details>


### [63] [Embedding Subspace Partitioning for Dynamic Multi-Objective Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30601)
*Shaobo Zhang,Alice Leung,Yunxiang Ren,Ping Liu,Yuchin Juan,Qianqi Shen,Benjamin Le,Jianqiang Shen,Chengming Jiang,Ko-Cheng Wang,Vidya Krishnamurthy,Caleb Johnson,Fedor Borisyuk,Luke Simon,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Embedding Subspace Partitioning (ESP)，一种将嵌入分解为任务感知子空间、并用可调权重加权子空间相似度的检索框架，使模型在服务时无需重训即可调整多目标权衡，并在 MS MARCO 基准与 LinkedIn 职位匹配平台上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统需同时优化语义相关性与参与度、收入等业务指标。主流双塔检索模型因效率优势被广泛采用，但将异质信号压缩为单一静态嵌入空间，导致训练完成后无法在服务阶段适应不断变化的目标优先级；同时多目标联合优化易引发目标间干扰，造成次优权衡。

Method: 提出 ESP 检索框架：将嵌入分解为任务感知子空间，用各子空间相似度的加权和替代单一内积，权重可在服务时调节。对 Transformer 双塔，利用模型原生的 EOS token 作为分段分隔符，配合分段感知的注意力掩码和位置编码重置，在单次前向传播中保证子空间隔离。服务阶段在单一拼接索引上执行 GPU 加速的穷举 kNN，避免多头方法所需的按目标 ANN 基础设施。

Result: 在基于 MS MARCO 构建的开源基准上，单个 ESP 模型可勾勒出较宽的 Pareto 前沿，在不同操作点上持续优于强多任务基线；在 LinkedIn 职位匹配平台（每周 7000 万以上用户）上，ESP 支持动态检索重配置，并带来关键业务指标的显著提升。

Conclusion: ESP 通过子空间划分与可调加权相似度，在保持双塔检索效率的同时实现了服务时多目标权衡的动态调整，克服了静态嵌入与目标干扰的局限，具备大规模工业部署价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems must optimize across competing objectives, balancing semantic relevance with business metrics such as engagement and revenue. While bi-encoders dominate large-scale retrieval due to their efficiency, they collapse these heterogeneous signals into a single static embedding space. This design creates a fundamental limitation: once trained, the retriever cannot adapt to shifting objective priorities at serving time without retraining. Moreover, joint optimization with multi-objective losses often induces interference between objectives, leading to suboptimal trade-offs. We propose Embedding Subspace Partitioning (ESP), a retrieval framework that decomposes the embedding into task-aware subspaces and replaces the single dot product with a weighted sum of per-subspace similarities, whose weights are tunable at serving time. For Transformer bi-encoders, ESP uses the model's native end-of-sequence token as a segment delimiter, with segment-aware attention masking and position encoding resets to guarantee subspace isolation in a single forward pass. Serving is performed via GPU-accelerated exhaustive kNN over one concatenated index, eliminating the need for per-objective Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) infrastructure required by multi-head approaches. We evaluate ESP on an open-source benchmark built from MS MARCO. A single ESP model traces a broad Pareto frontier, consistently outperforming strong multi-task baselines across diverse operating points. In LinkedIn's job matching platform (70M+ weekly users), ESP enabled dynamic retrieval reconfiguration and delivered significant key business metric lifts.

</details>


### [64] [Component Benchmark: Hierarchical Model Profiling for Large-scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30656)
*Dharak Kharod,Yuzhen Huang,Zhou Wang,Jackie Xu,Fuzail Khan,Jacky Zhou,Hao Yan,Lidong Zhao,Xizhou Feng,Yvonne Liu,Karthik Jayaraman,Praveen Ramachandran,Vishwa Karia,Yashasvi Makin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出组件级性能分析系统 CB，对异构推荐模型按子模块分层基准测试并提供树状交互可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐模型架构高度异构，混杂内存带宽受限算子、小型计算受限密集层、来自不规则类别特征的动态形状以及低算术强度操作，且模型迭代极快而工程师缺乏硬件执行特征的可见性。现有剖析工具只能给出端到端吞吐或算子级 trace，无法将性能归因到工程师真正关心的子模块。

Method: Component Benchmark（CB）：以子模块为单位独立刻画性能的分层剖析系统，核心是可扩展的基于子模块的基准测试框架与插件式架构，支持分层性能分析，并输出树状、可交互的可视化界面。

Result: 在常见开源模型上验证了 CB 的有效性；这些模型规模达 TB 级、运行在数千张 GPU 上、每日摄入 100B 条样本。CB 已被用于加速现代推荐模型的性能分析与优化。

Conclusion: CB 通过子模块级、分层的基准测试与可视化，弥补了传统剖析工具在异构推荐模型上的归因空白，为工程实践提供了可操作的性能洞察。

Abstract: Large-scale recommendation models pose distinct, under-explored profiling challenges. Most recommendation model architectures are structurally heterogeneous, intermixing memory-bandwidth-bound operations, small compute-bound dense layers, dynamic shapes from jagged categorical features, and low-arithmetic-intensity operations. Recommendation models evolve rapidly as modeling engineers experiment with compositions, often written without visibility into hardware execution characteristics. Standard profiling tools offer either end-to-end throughput or operator-level traces, but cannot attribute performance to the submodules that practitioners reason about. We present Component Benchmark (CB), a profiling system that independently characterizes each submodule performance in a hierarchical manner, providing a tree-structured, interactive visualization that brings performance clarity to ML practitioners. At its core, CB provides a simple yet extensible, submodule-based benchmarking framework with a plugin architecture that enables hierarchical performance analysis. These large-scale recommendation models are TB-scale, run on thousands of GPUs and ingest 100B examples per day. We demonstrate CB's effectiveness on common open sourced models and discuss how CB has been leveraged to accelerate modern recommendation model performance analysis and optimization.

</details>


### [65] [Recommendation World Models for Future-State Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30711)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Jianheng Tang,Zheng Lin,Jing Yang,Puzhen Wu,Zheng Xing,Victor C. M. Leung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 UA-TWM，一种效用锚定的世界模型接口，使已训练的序列推荐排序器能够评估并选择对用户未来反馈更有利的展示列表，在保持效用的同时实现目标导向决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐传统上只优化当下排序的物品，但每个展示列表还会影响用户后续反馈与状态。因此需要研究如何让已训练的排序器支持对未来后果的决策。

Method: UA-TWM 构建邻近列表动作、估计其目标相关后果，并在效用约束下选择替代列表；当无替代满足条件时回退到参考列表。含两种实例化：日志回放式结合效用与目标增益估计及校准的失败风险预测；闭环式采用一步状态-动作预测并在观测反馈后更新决策。

Result: 在 MovieLens-25M 与 KuaiRand-Pure 上跨十二种序列骨干验证迁移，并在 KuaiSim 中进行重复目标导向交互。接入该接口后，所有匹配的日志回放骨干的 Recall@20、NDCG@20 及未来状态对齐均得到提升。选择消融揭示了激进目标追求带来效用与风险代价，闭环诊断分离了动作条件预测的贡献。

Conclusion: 局部后果建模能够在已训练的序列排序器周围实现目标感知的选择，兼顾效用与未来反馈影响。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation optimizes which items to rank, while each displayed slate also shapes subsequent feedback and user state. We study how a trained ranker can support decisions about these future consequences. We introduce UA-TWM, a utility-anchored world-model interface that constructs nearby slate actions, estimates their target-relevant consequences, and selects an alternative subject to utility constraints. The reference slate serves as a fallback when no alternative qualifies. A logged-replay instantiation combines utility and target-gain estimates with calibrated failure-risk prediction; a closed-loop instantiation uses one-step state-action prediction and updates its decisions after observed feedback. We evaluate transfer across twelve sequential backbones on MovieLens-25M and KuaiRand-Pure, and repeated target-directed interaction in KuaiSim. Attaching the interface improves Recall@20, NDCG@20, and future-state alignment for every matched logged backbone. Selection ablations reveal the utility and risk costs of aggressive target pursuit, while closed-loop diagnostics isolate the contribution of action-conditioned prediction. Local consequence modeling thus enables target-aware selection around a trained sequential ranker.

</details>


### [66] [RecToolBench: Benchmarking Recommendation-Specific Tool Orchestration under Fuzzy User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30717)
*Xiao Chen,Yicheng Zhao,Yingying Wu,Zhendong Chu,Changyi Ma,Qingsong Wen,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 RecToolBench——一个基于MCP的基准，用于评测在模糊用户指令下的工具调用式推荐智能体，涵盖3个领域、13个MCP服务器、32个工具及1200+可执行任务，揭示工具编排是当前智能体推荐系统的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体推荐系统正从被动过滤引擎转向依赖外部工具的指令遵循智能体，但现有基准普遍假设用户意图明确、工具环境简化或仅考察孤立函数调用，缺乏对真实工具编排场景的探索。

Method: 构建基于Model Context Protocol（MCP）的基准RecToolBench：包含1200+可执行任务、3个推荐领域、13个MCP服务器、32个工具，覆盖单工具调用、并行调用、顺序工具链与混合编排；采用可扩展的"合成—模糊化—评判"（synthesize–fuzzify–judge）流水线生成可执行模糊推荐任务，并通过基于规则的执行检查与基于评分量规的LLM评估来评判智能体轨迹。

Result: 对代表性LLM的实验表明，语法合法的工具调用并不保证推荐成功；模型在语义参数落地、多步证据整合和基于依据的最终推荐上表现吃力，且随编排复杂度上升而显著恶化。

Conclusion: 模糊用户意图下的工具编排是智能体推荐系统的主要瓶颈；RecToolBench为相关评测提供了可扩展的任务生成与多维度评估框架，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic recommender systems are shifting recommender systems from passive filtering engines to instruction-following agents that use external tools to resolve user intent. However, existing benchmarks often assume explicit user intent, simplified tool environments, or isolated function calls, leaving realistic tool orchestration for recommendation underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose RecToolBench, a Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based benchmark for evaluating tool-using recommender agents under fuzzy user instructions. RecToolBench contains more than 1,200 executable tasks across three recommendation domains, 13 MCP servers, and 32 tools, spanning single-tool calls, parallel tool calls, sequential tool chains, and hybrid tool orchestration. We construct RecToolBench with a scalable synthesize--fuzzify--judge pipeline that generates executable fuzzy recommendation tasks, and evaluates agent trajectories using rule-based execution checks and rubric-based LLM evaluation. Experiments on representative LLMs show that syntactically valid tool calls do not guarantee successful recommendations. Models struggle with semantic parameter grounding, multi-step evidence integration, and grounded final recommendations, especially as orchestration complexity increases. Our results identify tool orchestration under fuzzy user intent as a major bottleneck for agentic recommender systems. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/ShawnChenn/RecToolBench.

</details>


### [67] [QReason: Query-Focused Decoupled Chain-of-Thought for Efficient Passage Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30904)
*Yang Zhang,Wenhan Liu,Qiannan Zhu,Mingming Li,Yuanfei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QReason 提出解耦式段落重排序框架，将“查询导向推理”与“窗口内段落相关性判断”分离：先用重写器一次性生成可复用的面向排序的推理查询，再由非推理重排序器在各滑动窗口间复用该推理，从而减少冗余推理与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的 listwise 重排序器结合思维链（CoT）推理虽能有效处理复杂查询，但采用滑动窗口策略时需对每个窗口重复生成高度相似的 CoT，导致大量冗余和高延迟。作者希望消除这种重复推理，同时保持复杂查询的排序效果。

Method: QReason 采用解耦框架：（1）专用重写器仅生成一次“面向排序的推理查询”，捕获查询核心意图，避免冗余推理；（2）该推理查询在所有窗口中被非推理重排序器复用。重写器的训练分两阶段：第一阶段用监督微调，借助语义证据的相关段落引导，产生深度接地、查询导向的 CoT；第二阶段用强化学习，使 CoT 生成同时对齐推理时设置与重排序目标，优化 listwise 指标和段落级判别能力，从而生成可复用的推理链。

Result: 在 BRIGHT 基准上的实验表明：QReason 显著减少冗余推理，排序性能与强推理型重排序器相当或更优，并优于现有查询重写模型。

Conclusion: 通过将推理查询生成与窗口级相关性评估解耦，QReason 在降低推理冗余和延迟的同时保持了具有竞争力的重排序性能，验证了“一次推理、多窗复用”范式在 LLM 重排序中的有效性。

Abstract: Passage reranking plays a crucial role in information retrieval by refining the ordering of candidate passages to better reflect relevance. Existing listwise LLM rerankers with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning can handle complex queries effectively, but they suffer from substantial redundancy and high latency due to sliding-window strategies, which repeatedly generate highly similar CoTs. To address this, we propose QReason, a decoupled framework that separates query-focused reasoning from window-specific passage relevance assessment. Specifically, QReason introduces a dedicated rewriter that generates a ranking-oriented reasoning query once, capturing the query's core intent while avoiding redundant reasoning, and then reuses it across all windows with a non-reasoning reranker. The rewriter is trained via a two-stage process that first uses supervised fine-tuning with relevant-passage guidance through semantic evidence to produce deeply grounded, query-focused CoTs. It then applies reinforcement learning to align CoT generation with both the inference-time setting and the reranking objective, optimizing listwise metrics and passage-level discrimination to produce reusable reasoning chains for reranking. Experiments on the BRIGHT benchmark demonstrate that QReason significantly reduces redundant reasoning, achieves ranking performance comparable to or better than strong reasoning-based rerankers, and outperforms existing query rewriting models.

</details>


### [68] [KuaFu: Compressing Long User Behavior into Understanding at Billion Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31045)
*Jiahao Hui,Lin Zhu,Yishen Hu,Jingdong Shu,Zetai Jiang,Xining Ran,Ben Tan,Yeshou Cai,Gong Chen,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KuaFu：以单个行为项为最小单元的统一行为压缩层，将每项压缩至2-4个token（宽度128-256），在四大生产画像任务上追平或超越未压缩单任务模型，吞吐提升37%-350%，节省190块GPU，并在腾讯广告推荐平台上线十个月，整体GMV提升1.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话代理、生成式推荐与个性化广告都依赖从原始行为理解用户。工业界普遍采用任务专用做法：为每个任务抽取相关子序列并训练专用模型。但生产中遇到两个瓶颈：(1) 即使过滤后单任务序列仍极长（内容兴趣摘要每用户数百条，序列化为prompt后达数万token）；(2) 画像需常规刷新（每周十亿用户、约100K QPM），在固定GPU预算下形成硬性吞吐下限。压缩虽属必需，但截断或粗粒度压缩会悄悄扭曲画像，引入四类幻觉（捏造、遗漏、日期错配、逻辑断裂），且缺乏对压缩表示本身的评估手段，只能在下游指标上表现为弥散式退化。

Method: 提出KuaFu统一行为压缩层，以单个行为项为最小单元。采用双轴投影器将每项压缩为2-4个、宽度128-256的token（token轴约10倍、宽度约20倍压缩；单项缓存由10KB降至0.5KB）。配套面向保真度的四阶段训练与分层中间评估。

Result: 在四个生产画像任务上，五项核心指标全部追平或超过未压缩的单任务生产模型；单GPU吞吐提升37%-350%，节省190块GPU。公开基准上同压缩率下几乎总能超过先前压缩器（域外MRQA最高+17.7 EM）；在RecBench上4B模型超过其8B对照1.90分。已在腾讯广告与推荐平台运行十个月，整体GMV提升1.37%。

Conclusion: 以行为项为最小单元的统一压缩层可在保证画像保真度的同时显著降低序列长度与计算开销，兼顾下游效果、吞吐与部署成本，并已在大规模工业场景验证其长期收益。

Abstract: Conversational agents, generative recommenders, and personalized advertising all rest on one capability: understanding each user from raw behavior. Prevailing industrial practice is task-specific: for each task, a relevant subsequence is extracted from the full history and a dedicated model trained on it. In production it hits two bottlenecks. First, even after filtering, a single-task sequence stays extremely long: content-interest summarization reads several hundred items per user, tens of thousands of tokens once serialized as prompt text. Second, profiles are refreshed routinely: a billion users weekly, roughly 100K QPM in aggregate, which under a fixed GPU budget sets a hard throughput floor. Compression is therefore mandatory, yet truncation or coarse compression can silently distort the profile, introducing four hallucination types (fabrication, omission, date misattribution, broken logic) that, with no way to evaluate the compressed representation itself, surface only as diffuse degradation in downstream metrics. We present KuaFu, a unified behavior-compression layer whose minimal unit is one behavior item. A two-axis projector compresses each item into 2-4 tokens of width 128-256 (about 10x along the token axis, 20x along width; per-item cache 10 KB to 0.5 KB), with fidelity-oriented four-stage training and layered intermediate evaluation. Across four production profiling tasks it matches or exceeds uncompressed single-task production models on all five headline metrics, raises per-GPU throughput by 37%-350%, and saves 190 GPUs. On public benchmarks it nearly always beats prior compressors at the same compression ratio (up to +17.7 EM on out-of-domain MRQA); on RecBench, a 4B model surpasses its 8B counterpart by 1.90 points. KuaFu has run on the Tencent advertising and recommendation platform for ten months, lifting overall GMV by 1.37%.

</details>


### [69] [SPADE: Escaping the Popularity-Similarity Frontier to Measure Serendipitous Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31164)
*Tobias Vente,Maarten Peirsman,Noah Daniëls,Hannu Toivonen,Bart Goethals*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出SPADE（Serendipitous Pareto Distance Evaluation）离线评估指标，将物品映射到二维空间构建用户特定的帕累托前沿，通过计算正确推荐测试集物品到该边界的最小欧氏距离平均值来度量意外发现性，在五个数据集和五个基线算法上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统需要工程化地设计"意外发现性"（serendipity）以促进主动探索、打破可预测的消费循环。但现有离线超越准确率（beyond-accuracy）指标存在缺陷：它们要么只孤立地考察历史相似性，要么只考察全局流行度，无法同时兼顾相似性、流行度与真实用户相关性。

Method: SPADE将所有物品映射到二维空间，直接为用户计算由"最流行"与"历史最相似"物品构成的帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）。最终的意外发现性分数仅针对被正确推荐的测试集物品，计算其到该前沿的最小欧氏距离并取平均。该设计同时纳入相似性、流行度与真实用户相关性三个维度。

Result: 在五个数据集和五个基线算法上对SPADE进行评估，结果确认其有效性：该指标能够成功防止算法利用无关或非个性化推荐来"钻空子"（exploit）超越准确率指标，可靠地识别出真正的意外发现。

Conclusion: SPADE通过二维帕累托前沿的距离度量，弥补了现有超越准确率指标仅考察相似性或流行度单一维度的不足，为意外发现性提供了更严格的离线评估手段，能稳健地隔离出真实的意外发现推荐。

Abstract: Recommender systems engineer serendipity to foster active exploration and break predictable consumption cycles. The problem with existing offline beyond-accuracy metrics is that they often either isolate historical similarity or global popularity. We aim to design an evaluation metric that examines similarity, popularity, and actual user relevance. To achieve this, we introduce SPADE (Serendipitous Pareto Distance Evaluation). SPADE maps all items into a two-dimensional space to directly calculate a user-specific Pareto frontier of maximally popular and historically similar items. The final serendipity score is then computed by averaging the minimum Euclidean distance from this boundary strictly for the correctly recommended test-set items. Evaluating SPADE across five datasets and five baseline algorithms confirms its effectiveness; our results show that the metric successfully prevents algorithms from exploiting beyond-accuracy measures with irrelevant or non-personalized recommendations, reliably isolating serendipitous discoveries.

</details>


### [70] [AgentRecommender: LLM Agents Enable Customizable Recommender Systems on the User Side](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31166)
*Ryoma Sato*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentRecommender，利用 LLM 智能体的调研能力与内部知识，无需额外数据即可灵活构建用户侧推荐系统，使推荐系统摆脱平台利益束缚、贴合个人偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统面向平台开发，导致点击诱饵、信息茧房、假新闻传播等问题，这些现象有利于平台锁定用户却给用户带来困扰。用户侧推荐系统被视为新范式，让用户不再受平台利益摆布，但构建难度高，尤其为个人定制时还需额外数据。

Method: 提出 AgentRecommender：借助 LLM 智能体的调研（investigation）能力与内部知识，在不依赖额外数据的情况下，灵活搭建用户侧推荐系统，让用户能轻松创建符合自身偏好的推荐系统。

Result: 无需额外数据即可构建用户侧推荐系统；用户可便捷创建个性化、贴合自身偏好的推荐系统。（摘要未给出具体量化实验结论）

Conclusion: AgentRecommender 通过 LLM 智能体降低了用户侧推荐系统的构建门槛，免除额外数据依赖，为用户自主掌控推荐、摆脱平台利益主导提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Recommender systems have traditionally been developed for platforms. However, this has given rise to many phenomena that may be advantageous for platform lock-in but are a nuisance to users, such as clickbait, filter bubbles, and the spread of fake news. Recently, user-side recommender systems have been proposed as a new paradigm for solving this problem. If users deploy their own recommender systems, they are no longer at the mercy of the platform's interests. However, building a user-side recommender system is not trivial; in particular, customizing one for oneself requires additional data. We propose AgentRecommender, a method that leverages the investigation capability and internal knowledge of LLM agents to flexibly build user-side recommender systems without additional data. AgentRecommender allows users to easily create recommender systems tailored to their own preferences.

</details>


### [71] [Enriching Sequential Recommendation with Graph Laplacian Positional Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31253)
*Ekaterina Trushkova,Artur Gimranov,Anton Lysenko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出用基于物品共现图的拉普拉斯位置嵌入替换SASRec中的可学习序数位置嵌入，在四个公开序列推荐基准上多数排序指标取得提升，并可与强位置/时间编码基线竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐器通常依赖可学习的位置嵌入来编码用户交互顺序，作者质疑这种序数信号是否可以被从物品空间导出的结构信号所替代。
