We introduce Stackelberg Program Optimization (SPO), an LLM-based framework for discovering adaptive executable destroy-repair programs. SPO conditions operator decisions on a compact LNS state, allowing state-dependent behavior to emerge through program discovery, and organizes destroy-repair discovery as a Stackelberg interaction over program space that reflects their asymmetric dependency. Role-specific credits evaluate destroy programs as leaders and repair programs as conditional follower responses, guiding a coupled optimization process that combines LLM generator learning with population-based evolutionary search over programs. Experiments on the traveling salesperson problem and capacitated vehicle routing problem show that SPO outperforms strong baselines across a broad range of settings and generalizes beyond the discovery scale to larger instances and benchmark sets. Behavioral analyses further demonstrate state-dependent operator behavior and coupled destroy-repair improvement during discovery.

</details>


### [140] [DIAL: Position-Debiased LLM Judges with Adaptive Human Preference Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31215)
*Zesheng Cai,Yingqi Fan,Sichang Chen,Jin-Hong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DIAL统一框架，利用大量LLM比较与有限人类比较，分离评审位置效应、学习去偏后的LLM偏好共享结构，并自适应校准至人类偏好，同时提供理论保证与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评审虽可实现可扩展评估，但其判断对响应顺序敏感（位置效应），且即使去除位置效应后仍会系统性地偏离人类偏好，亟需统一方法同时解决去偏与对齐问题。

Method: DIAL结合丰富的LLM比较与有限人类比较：(1) 识别潜在LLM偏好、位置效应与人类校准；(2) 自适应估计，在LLM锚定与有限人类证据间平衡；(3) 对校准后人类偏好进行固定权重不确定性量化。实验中在受控模拟与三个人类偏好基准上分别评估位置去偏与人类对齐，并收集21个LLM评审在两种展示顺序下的超41万条判断。

Result: DIAL对不平衡响应顺序保持稳健，在有限标签下实现强人类对齐排名，并在LLM信息不完美时自适应向人类证据靠拢；同时构建了含41万+判断的LLM评审偏差与异质性资源。

Conclusion: DIAL统一了LLM评审的位置去偏与人类偏好校准，兼具理论识别、自适应估计与不确定性量化，为LLM评审偏差、异质性与人类对齐研究提供了方法与数据资源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) as a judge enable scalable evaluation, but their judgments can be sensitive to response order and, even after removing such position effects, can still diverge systematically from human preferences.We introduce DIAL, a unified framework that combines abundant LLM comparisons with limited human comparisons to separate judge-specific position effects, learn shared structure in position-debiased LLM preferences, and adaptively calibrate that structure toward the human preference target. Theoretically, we study three aspects of DIAL: (i) identification of latent LLM preferences, position effects, and human calibration; (ii) adaptive estimation that balances LLM anchoring against limited human evidence; and (iii) fixed-weight uncertainty quantification for the calibrated human preference. Empirically, we evaluate position debiasing and human alignment separately in controlled simulations and on three human-preference benchmarks, showing that DIAL remains robust to unbalanced response order, achieves strong human-aligned rankings with limited labels, and adapts toward human evidence when LLM information is imperfect. Our real-data study collects over 410K judgments from 21 LLM judges in both display orders, providing a resource for future studies of LLM-judge bias, heterogeneity, and human alignment.

</details>


### [141] [G2MAF: Test-Time Gradient Guidance for Multi-Agent Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31286)
*Guowei Zou,Haitao Wang,Guoxin Wang,Zhiquan Chen,Beiwen Zhang,Guojie Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出G2MAF：一种离线多智能体强化学习的测试时联合策略精化框架，用全局归一化的投影critic梯度协调各智能体修正，保持动作可行且贴近冻结策略提议，24个MPE/SMAC任务中20个获得提升（MPE平均相对增益9.2%，SMAC 8.9%），推理延迟仅增约6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL从固定数据集中学习协作策略，部署时策略被冻结。这种冻结策略通常只提出单一联合动作并直接执行，属于一次性部署（one-shot deployment），容易锁定次优提议，即使行为数据中仍支持更好的邻近替代动作。因此需要在测试时对联合策略进行精化优化。

Method: G2MAF（Gradient Guided Multi-Agent Flow）是一个测试时联合策略精化框架：施加一个全局归一化、投影后的critic梯度，用以引导并协调所有智能体的修正；同时约束动作既保持可行（feasible），又贴近冻结策略给出的初始提议。

Result: 在24个MPE与SMAC设置上评测，其标准变体（canonical variant）提升了20个冻结设置，MPE平均相对增益9.2%，SMAC平均相对增益8.9%，模型推理延迟仅增加约6%。

Conclusion: 通过测试时的梯度引导联合动作精化，可在几乎不增加推理开销的前提下，显著改善离线MARL冻结策略的部署性能，说明一次性部署并非最优，测试时精化是有效且低成本的改进方向。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) learns cooperative policies from fixed datasets without further environment interaction and a learned policy is frozen at deployment. Such a frozen policy typically proposes a single joint action and executes it directly at deployment time. However, this one-shot deployment often commits to a suboptimal proposal, even when better nearby alternatives remain consistent with the behavior data. To address this issue, we propose Gradient Guided Multi Agent Flow (G2MAF), a refinement framework for optimizing joint policies at test-time. G2MAF applies one globally normalized, projected critic gradient to guide and coordinate all agents' corrections while keeping the action both feasible and close to the frozen policy proposal. Across 24 MPE and SMAC settings, its canonical variant improves 20 frozen settings, with mean relative gains of 9.2% on MPE and 8.9% on SMAC, with model inference latency increased by about 6% only.

</details>


### [142] [Mutable Transcripts: Mitigating Context Pollution through Editable Conversation State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31354)
*Dan Barry,Andrew Hines*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"可变对话记录"交互范式,允许用户通过自然语言编辑请求修改历史轮次,将对话记录从被动记录转变为可编辑的状态表示,以缓解动态意图与静态记录之间的错配及上下文污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM聊天系统把对话历史视为不可变的轮次序列,并以此定义模型工作上下文。但真实交互中的用户意图是动态演化的,会通过纠错、细化和约束变化而改变。动态意图与静态记录之间的错配会导致上下文污染:过时或无关信息持续存在并影响后续回复。

Method: 提出可变对话记录(mutable transcripts)范式,支持用户以自然语言编辑请求修订先前轮次,使历史被更新而非仅追加,将记录重构为对话状态的可编辑表示。实现了一个将记录级修订集成到标准聊天界面的原型系统,并通过受控用户研究(n=17)和代表性交互场景的记录分析进行评估。

Result: 在清晰度、信心和易用性等指标上,参与者显著偏好可变对话记录而非标准聊天,同时重启对话的意图降低。对代表性用户研究对话的记录分析表明,可变记录能够缩短对话长度并消除保留的过时上下文。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明,用户驱动的对话历史修订可提升交互质量,并有助于维持更贴合当前用户意图的表示。源码与原型见 https://github.com/QxLabIreland/ReChat。

Abstract: Contemporary large language model (LLM) chat systems treat conversation history as an immutable sequence of turns that defines the model's working context. However, user intent in real interactions is not static: it evolves through correction, refinement, and shifting constraints. This mismatch between dynamic intent and static transcripts can result in context pollution, where outdated or irrelevant information persists and continues to influence subsequent responses. We introduce mutable transcripts, a new interaction paradigm that enables users to revise prior turns through natural language edit requests, allowing the conversation history itself to be updated rather than appended. This reframes the transcript from a passive record into an editable representation of conversational state. We present a working prototype that integrates transcript-level revision into a standard chat interface and evaluate its feasibility through a controlled user study (n=17) and an illustrative transcript analysis of representative interaction scenarios. Participants significantly preferred mutable transcripts over standard chat across measures of clarity, confidence, and ease of use, with reduced intent to restart conversations. Transcript analysis of representative user study conversations shows that mutable transcripts can reduce conversation length and eliminate obsolete retained context. These findings provide initial evidence that user-driven revision of conversational history can improve interaction quality and help maintain a more current representation of user intent. The source code and prototype can be accessed at https://github.com/QxLabIreland/ReChat

</details>


### [143] [Completed Pairs Hide Capped Failures: A ReVerPi Case Study of Selective Context Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31381)
*Guangzhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以 ReVerPi 实验为例，研究"上下文投影"（用紧凑可寻址摘录替代旧工具观测）在减少重复输入与增加检索轮次之间的权衡，并借此讨论停止规则、已知有界失败、未执行对照臂与资源聚合等方法学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文投影可压缩重复输入，但可能引入额外的证据检索轮次，二者构成权衡；同时现有评测中停止规则、对照臂执行方式与资源统计口径会对结论产生实质影响，需要以方法学案例的方式加以考察。

Method: 构建 Pi 扩展 ReVerPi，采用归档观测与"完全/投影"配对的续写；开展 86 次运行、641 个模型请求的源码阅读实验，对 15 对完成的配对进行比较，并对 27 个边界运行补充分析，同时区分选择器的拟合与评估阶段进行资源与成功率统计。

Result: 15 对完成配对成功率相同（各 12/15）；恢复全部 27 个边界运行后，投影减完全的成功差被界定在 −9 到 +1 个任务之间。一个被选择的投影续写虽成功检索归档文本，却耗尽 12 个请求，其完全对照仅用 3 个请求完成。在 11 对共同正确的成功分层内，投影使聚合逻辑 token 减少 25%，但配对中位数 token 增加 29%，总后缀请求从 35 升至 55。区分拟合与评估后，选择器在其 4 个拟合配对之外多产生 1 次失败，并在 13 次可比运行中多用 8.6% 逻辑 token。

Conclusion: 该结论仅适用于所记录的实验框架，不构成对无限制 Pi 的群体非劣性或优越性主张；评测应保留所有干预边界、独立于第一臂完成情况执行两个分配臂，并同时报告完成率、交互次数与 token 支出。

Abstract: Context projection replaces older tool observations with compact, addressable excerpts, reducing repeated input while potentially adding evidence-retrieval turns. We study this trade-off in ReVerPi, a Pi extension with archived observations and matched full/projected continuations. In an 86-run source-reading campaign with 641 model requests, the 15 completed pairs show identical success: 12/15 per arm. Twelve further boundary runs stop, with the runner suppressing the companion whenever the first arm fails to complete. Restoring all 27 boundary runs bounds projected-minus-full success between $-$9 and +1 tasks. One omitted, selector-chosen projected continuation successfully retrieves archive text yet exhausts twelve requests; its full counterpart answers in three. The eleven jointly correct pairs form a fully observed success stratum within this recorded frame: projection reduces aggregate logical tokens by 25%, while increasing the median pair's tokens by 29% and total suffix requests from 35 to 55. Separating fitting from evaluation changes the selector's apparent tie: outside its four fitting pairs, it incurs one extra failure and 8.6% more logical tokens over thirteen comparable runs. This methodological case study connects stopping rules, known bounded failures, unexecuted companions, and resource aggregation. Its findings concern the recorded campaign, rather than population noninferiority or superiority over unrestricted Pi. Evaluations should retain every intervention boundary, execute both allocated arms independently of the first arm's completion, and report completion alongside interaction and token expenditure.

</details>


### [144] [Compress What You See, Not What You Say: Anchored Context Distillation for Latent-Observation Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31430)
*Zhensheng Zou,Guoqing Wang,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LOHA上下文布局与ACD训练方法，将旧工具观测压缩为软token并保留最近K条文本观测，在压缩上下文的同时保持动作关键信息与智能体行为，于SWE-bench Verified上以43%–57%的上下文缩减取得接近甚至更优的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体的上下文被工具观测主导，长交互历史维护成本高。已有压缩方法会丢失后续动作所需信息，而将智能体适配到软token表示又可能破坏其原有行为，因此需要在压缩上下文的同时保留动作关键信息与智能体行为。

Method: 方法由两部分组成：LOHA是一种上下文布局，将较早的工具观测压缩为软token，同时以文本形式保留智能体自身回合与最近K条观测，从而对历史信息提供紧凑访问、对近期内容提供精确访问；ACD是一种训练方法，将基座模型在完整文本上的预测蒸馏到潜在视图，同时在纯文本输入上把行为锚定到同一基座模型，实现潜在读取并约束行为漂移。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，K=3使每次调用上下文缩减43%（Qwen3-4B）与57%（SWE-Master-4B-RL），解决率分别为12.1%与21.8%，而未压缩基座为14.5%与27.5%；单次运行的recency sweep在K=8时达到14.4%与23.0%，窗口越大越有利于任务表现而非压缩。32K token限制下，Qwen3在K=3时于199个实例子集上解决21.1%，而同一适配智能体使用完整文本仅11.1%；在并发单GPU服务中，其实例吞吐量为完整文本智能体的1.9倍。

Conclusion: LOHA+ACD能在显著压缩上下文的同时基本保持智能体行为与动作关键信息，并在受限上下文预算与高并发服务场景下相较完整文本方案获得明显优势。

Abstract: Tool observations dominate the context of software-engineering agents, making long interaction histories costly to maintain. Existing context compression methods can discard information needed by later actions, while adapting agents to soft-token representations can compromise their original behavior. To reduce context while preserving action-critical information and agent behavior, we combine Latent Observations, Hard Actions (LOHA), a context layout that separates compressed history from text needed for exact reference, with Anchored Context Distillation (ACD), a training method that enables latent reading while constraining behavioral drift. LOHA compresses older tool observations into soft tokens while retaining the agent's own turns and the last K observations in text, providing compact access to historical information and exact access to recent content. To enable the agent to use this representation, ACD distills the base model's full-text predictions into the latent view while anchoring its behavior on plain-text inputs to the same base model. On SWE-bench Verified, K=3 reduces context per call by 43% for Qwen3-4B and 57% for SWE-Master-4B-RL, with resolve rates of 12.1% and 21.8% versus 14.5% and 27.5% for their uncompressed bases. A single-run recency sweep reaches 14.4% and 23.0% at K=8, with larger windows generally favoring task performance over compression. Under a 32K-token limit, Qwen3 with K=3 resolves 21.1% of a 199-instance subset versus 11.1% for the same adapted agent using full text. In concurrent single-GPU serving, it achieves 1.9 times that full-text agent's instance throughput.

</details>


### [145] [Segment-Level Agentic Topic Modeling for Improved Data Exploration and Resource Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31460)
*Myeongjun Erik Jang,Antonios Georgiadis,Sae Young Moon,Fran Silavong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SeLATM 框架，通过段落级主题生成与代理反馈循环的主题精炼，解决 LLM 主题模型在资源消耗、主题粒度与主题分布等方面的不足，在保持优越性能的同时显著降低 LLM 资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 主题模型虽能生成更自然、可读的主题，但其"先生成主题再分配主题"的范式存在固有缺陷：无法给出文档的主题分布、主题过宽或过窄、资源消耗随文档数量与长度增长。工业场景需高质量深度分析且处理海量文档，因此亟需改进。

Method: 提出 SeLATM 框架，采用段落级主题生成（segment-level topic generation），并借助代理反馈循环（agentic feedback loops）进行主题精炼。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，与基于主题分配过程的方法相比，SeLATM 显著减少 LLM 资源消耗，同时保持更优性能。

Conclusion: SeLATM 以段落级生成与代理反馈精炼替代主题分配范式，兼顾效率与质量，更契合工业级大规模文档分析需求。

Abstract: Topic modeling is an effective technique for discovering hidden themes within documents and is widely used in text mining and data analysis across a variety of industry sectors. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based topic models have been emerged that prompt LLMs to generate topics then assign the topics to documents, producing more natural and human-readable topics than conventional topic modeling algorithms. However, the nature of topic assignment process causes certain drawbacks, such as the incapability to produce topic distributions over a document, too broad or narrow topics, and high resource consumption, which increases with the number and length of of documents being assigned topics. These issues are particularly critical for industrial applications, which require high-quality, in-depth analysis and the processing of large volumes of documents. In this context, this paper introduces a framework called SeLATM, which addresses these concerns by employing segment-level topic generation and topic refinement through agentic feedback loops. Experimental results on various datasets demonstrate that SeLATM significantly reduces the LLM resources compared to methods based on topic assignment process, while maintaining superior performance.

</details>


### [146] [Game Arena: Strategic LLM Evaluation in Competitive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31473)
*Bovard Doerschuk-Tiberi,Yao Yan,Justin Chiu,Hann Wang,Timothy Chung,Martyna Plomecka,John Schultz,Jon Lipovetz,Clayton Drazner,Yuchen Zhuang,Jaimie Hwang,Nate Keating,Riley Jones,Andrew Lee,Oran Kelly,Ian Gemp,Michael Aaron,Laurel Prince,Kate Larson,Jeff Moser,Harrison Jobe,Chad Woodford,Siqi Liu,Andrew Wang,Bo Chang,Christopher D'Mello,Diane Chaleff,Addison Howard,Johnny Yip,Chuck Sugnet,Antonio Gulli,Meghan O'Connell,Will Cukierski,Nenad Tomasev,Dima Yeroshenko,Kinjal Parekh,Roxanne Daniel,Marc Lanctot,Domino Weir,Elsa Dong,Daniel Hennes,Melissa Nalubwama,Robert Fraser,Ryan Trostle,Jun Peng,Tom Mason,Lloyd Hightower,Chiamaka Chukwuka,Yuexiang Zhai,Phoebe Kirk,Yi Su,Yuting Han,Jie Ren,Chris Prichard,Sahand Sharifzadeh,Karim Hakimzadeh,DJ Sterling,Meg Risdal,Kate Olszewska,Ya Xu,Orhan Firat,Minmin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Kaggle Game Arena，一个通过竞技游戏评估LLM的开放平台，避免静态基准的性能饱和；首批包含国际象棋、扑克、狼人杀三类环境，并给出完整竞赛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准易出现性能饱和，难以持续区分不断进化的LLM能力。

Method: 构建可扩展的游戏竞技基础设施，让模型在结构化环境中进行对抗赛；设计覆盖完全信息（国际象棋）、不完全信息（扑克）与多人博弈（狼人杀）的三类试点环境，并配套评估指标与可复现的规模化成对评测。

Result: 在三个试点环境中组织跨模型完整竞赛，验证平台能系统考察模型的策略规划、适应性与不确定性下的稳健性；依托真实对抗与大规模评测保证可复现性与透明性。

Conclusion: 游戏竞技场随模型能力提升而自然增加对抗强度，可泛化到新游戏与变体，为LLM评估提供持续有效的开放范式。

Abstract: We introduce Kaggle Game Arena, an open and ever-expanding platform to evaluate large language models (LLMs) through competitive games. Different from static benchmarks, game arena enables models to play head-to-head matchups in structured environments where the gameplay strength naturally increases as models evolve, preventing performance saturation. This technical report details the infrastructure behind Game Arena and describes the three pilot game environments: Chess, Poker, and Werewolf. These environments span perfect information, imperfect information, and multiplayer game settings, enabling a systematic study of models' strategic planning, adaptation, and robustness under uncertainty. For each game, we provide a detailed description of the environment, evaluation metrics, and results from running full competitions across models. Through robust infrastructure and large-scale ground-truth based evaluation, Game Arena ensures reproducibility, transparency and generalizability to new games and variants over time.

</details>


### [147] ["AI is (not) the new...": A Diagnostic Analogy Framework for Generative AI's Cultural Impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31482)
*Rida Qadri,Vinodkumar Prabhakaran,Remi Denton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，将生成式AI介入分解为三个坐标（认识论场域、治理逻辑、技术机制），用以区分结构性文化后果与偶然性后果，并据此分析信息发现与知识合成领域的文化效应及治理杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学者与政策制定者常用印刷术、蒸汽机、电力等历史类比来理解生成式AI带来的文化与知识基础设施变革。但这类类比往往未能明确说明所比较的是技术的哪一属性，因而不精确的类比会导致不精确的治理——干预措施针对了系统的错误属性。因此需要一个更精确的分析框架。

Method: 提出一个诊断框架，将每一项技术介入分解为三个坐标：(1) 认识论场域（技术发生作用的位置）；(2) 治理逻辑（技术组织其对象的组织原则）；(3) 技术机制（治理逻辑得以实例化的技术途径）。以此区分由机制本身导致的结构性文化后果与仍开放于设计与制度选择的偶然性后果。

Result: 将该框架应用于信息发现与知识合成两个领域，展示了从"索引性"到"推断"、从"编辑权威"到"统计共识"的转变如何产生具体且可追溯的文化效应，并揭示出整体性类比所掩盖的治理杠杆。

Conclusion: 精确分解技术介入的三个坐标有助于识别哪些文化后果是结构性的、哪些是偶然可选择的，从而为生成式AI的知识与文化治理提供更精准的干预依据，避免基于模糊历史类比而针对错误属性进行治理。

Abstract: Generative AI is reshaping the cultural infrastructures through which knowledge is found, synthesized, and held accountable. To make sense of this shift, scholars and policymakers reach for historical analogies of technologies such as the printing press, steam power or electricity. But these comparisons are typically imprecise about which property of the technology carries the comparison, and imprecise analogies produce imprecise governance by designing interventions against the wrong property of the system. This paper offers a diagnostic framework for analyzing how generative AI can transform epistemic and cultural practice. This paper offers a diagnostic framework for analyzing how generative AI can transform epistemic and cultural practice. We decompose each intervention into three coordinates: the epistemic site at which a technology acts, the governing logic by which it organizes its object, and the technical mechanism through which the logic is instantiated. This framework allows us to distinguish between structural cultural consequences, which follow from the mechanism itself, from contingent ones, which remain open to design and institutional choice. Applying the framework to information discovery and knowledge synthesis, we show how the shift from indexicality to inference and from editorial authority to statistical consensus produces specific, traceable cultural effects and reveals governance levers that gestalt analogy obscures.

</details>


### [148] [UQ-LOB: Uncertainty-Aware Limit Order Book Mid-Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31491)
*Derrick Gilchrist Edward Manoharan,Eljas Linna,Kestutis Baltakys,Hao Dong,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UQ-LOB，一个轻量、与编码器无关的不确定性量化模块，可附加于任意预训练LOB编码器，通过上下文集合条件化预测，输出校准的概率分布并提供标量置信度以支持选择性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度限价订单簿（LOB）预测器多为点预测，仅输出方向或位移，无法指示哪些预测可信，缺乏不确定性刻画，难以支撑算法的选择性预测与风险控制。

Method: 借鉴attentive neural processes思想，将每个预测条件化于近期已完成窗口构成的上下文集合；模块与编码器无关、轻量。UQ-regression变体输出未来tick位移的校准高斯分布，UQ-classification变体输出down/up/stationary的类别分布，两者均暴露标量置信度（预测信噪比或类别概率）。

Result: 在7种加密资产、52亿LOB事件、5/10/15秒预测期上评测：UQ-regression达到接近标称的68%区间覆盖率；仅保留置信度最高的10%预测时，方向性macro F1在UQ-regression提升0.11-0.15、UQ-classification提升0.05-0.11，且在所有预测期均成立。在幅度较大且经济意义显著的价格变动上，最紧置信层在5秒预测期的方向F1达0.88（下跌）与0.83（上涨）。

Conclusion: UQ-LOB以极低附加成本为通用LOB编码器提供校准的不确定性估计与可用置信度，显著提升选择性预测下的方向性准确率，验证了不确定性量化在高频LOB预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Forecasting short-horizon mid-price movements from limit order book (LOB) data is central to algorithmic trading, yet most deep LOB forecasters are point predictors: they output a direction or a displacement, but never indicate which of their forecasts can be trusted. We introduce UQ-LOB, a lightweight, encoder-agnostic uncertainty quantification module that attaches to any pretrained LOB encoder and, in the spirit of attentive neural processes, conditions each forecast on a context set of recently completed windows whose outcomes are already realised. The UQ-regression variant outputs a calibrated Gaussian over the future tick displacement, while the UQ-classification variant outputs a categorical distribution over down/up/stationary. Both expose a scalar confidence (predicted signal-to-noise ratio or class probability) that supports selective prediction. On 5.2 billion LOB events across seven cryptocurrency assets and horizons of 5, 10 and 15 seconds, UQ-regression attains near-nominal 68% interval coverage, and restricting to the most confident 10% of predictions raises directional macro F1 by 0.11-0.15 for UQ-regression and 0.05-0.11 for UQ-classification, at every horizon. On large, economically meaningful moves, the tightest confidence tier reaches a directional F1 of 0.88 (down) and 0.83 (up) at the 5-second horizon.

</details>


### [149] [Prompt Minimization: Reducing Input Redundancy Without Sacrificing Output Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31505)
*Marius F. R. Juston,Kevin A. Karim,Jonathan Gao,Kevin C. Li,Rudhi Bashambu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“提示最小化”（prompt minimization）这一概念，即在不损失输出保真度的前提下，将提示压缩至最小且信息密度最高的形式，并提出三种框架来识别与评估最小提示，证明其输出效果常可与更长提示相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的提示设计大多依赖启发式经验和临时性尝试，缺乏系统性方法。实践中，过长的提示（如不必要的整篇文档或代码库上下文）会带来额外的计算开销与推理延迟，并可能损害模型的推理能力与准确率；理论上，多种提示可产生等价输出，说明输入空间存在高度冗余，这引发了对“哪些信息是激发特定模型行为所必需”这一根本问题的思考。

Method: 提出“提示最小化”这一研究课题，旨在将提示缩减为最小且信息密度最高的形式，同时保持输出保真度。具体地，作者设计了三种变体框架，用于识别和评估最小提示。

Result: 实验表明，最小提示所产生的输出往往与更长提示的输出具有可比性。

Conclusion: 该发现为高效提示工程提供了新的方向，并加深了对大语言模型输入压缩机制的理解。

Abstract: Despite the growing capabilities of large language models (LLMs), prompt design remains largely heuristic and ad hoc. This project will explore $\textit{prompt minimization}$, the process of reducing prompts to their smallest, most information-dense form while preserving output fidelity. Practically, shorter prompts reduce computational overhead and inference latency, especially when large contexts, such as entire documents or codebases, are included unnecessarily. Further, longer prompts can damage LLM reasoning and accuracy. Theoretically, the existence of multiple prompts yielding equivalent outputs suggests a high degree of redundancy in the input space, raising fundamental questions about what information is essential to elicit specific model behaviors. We propose three variant frameworks to identify and evaluate minimal prompts and demonstrate that minimal prompts often produce outputs comparable to those of their longer counterparts. These findings suggest new directions for efficient prompt engineering and deepen our understanding of input compression in LLMs.

</details>


### [150] [A Flow Matching Framework for Neural Representational Dissimilarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31544)
*Zeyuan Ye,Xue-Xin Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用深度生成模型中的流匹配（flow matching）框架统一多种神经表征距离度量，证明这些距离可视为不同速度约束下的Jeffreys散度，并据此改进估计与新度量的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表征差异性用于量化神经响应分布之间的差异，对跨刺激、脑区、任务和模型比较神经编码至关重要；但常用距离度量基于不同假设且需分别估计，缺乏统一理解与构造原则。

Method: 作者将多种距离度量纳入流匹配框架，证明它们对应于不同速度约束下的Jeffreys散度，并利用流匹配方法进行估计。

Result: 流匹配在估计涉及复杂分布和连续变量的距离时具有优势，并且该框架能够在有原则的方式下设计新的距离度量。

Conclusion: 流匹配为理解、估计和设计神经表征差异性度量提供了统一方法。

Abstract: Neural representational dissimilarity quantifies differences between neural response distributions, and is essential for comparing neural codes across stimuli, brain areas, tasks, and models. Commonly used distance metrics involve different assumptions and are estimated with separate methods. Here, we show that a variety of distance metrics can be unified under a flow matching framework developed in deep generative models. That is, these distances arise as Jeffreys divergences under different velocity constraints. We find that flow matching has advantages for estimating distances involving complicated distributions and continuous variables. Furthermore, this framework enables the design of new distance metrics in a principled way. Together, flow matching provides a unified approach for understanding, estimating, and designing neural representational dissimilarity metrics.

</details>


### [151] [DeepEdu-v1: Efficient and Scalable Agentic LLMs for Vietnamese Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31568)
*Quang Nguyen,Hieu Nguyen,Hien Hoang,Toan Pham,Cong Tran,Nam Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出DeepEdu-v1，一个面向越南教育的AI辅导系统，基于SCALE框架，通过长上下文推理引擎（聚类粒度的token选择）与自改进智能体层（从历史交互中提炼经验手册而非微调），在消费级GPU上实现约2倍TTFT加速，并将复杂任务准确率从70.0%提升至79.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在发展地区（如越南）部署AI辅导面临两难：云助手（如ChatGPT）将敏感学生数据传至境外服务器，违反越南第53号法令等数据主权法规，且基于西方中心语料预训练，与本国教材课程体系不匹配，本地知识零散且频繁幻觉；自托管开源模型虽能保证数据本地化，但后训练量化只能压缩静态权重，长辅导上下文中的动态KV cache与prefill延迟仍导致消费级GPU显存溢出与响应缓慢，同时模型对区域特定内容仍持续幻觉。

Method: 提出DeepEdu-v1，建立在SCALE（Self-improving Context-Aware Learning Engine）框架之上，含两项创新：其一，长上下文推理引擎将token选择从每个子块粒度摊销到每个聚簇粒度；其二，自改进的智能体层通过持续从过往交互中整理出经过验证的playbook，替代微调，从而随可信本地知识积累逐步降低对主导语言先验的依赖。

Result: 长上下文检索时，检索调用次数比当前最先进的选择性注意力基线少7.7倍，prefill延迟（TTFT）降低约35%，同时任务准确率持平或提升。在部署配置下，DeepEdu相较标准vLLM服务实现近2倍TTFT加速，复杂任务的智能体准确率从70.0%提升至79.5%，在金融推理与交互式智能体基准上取得最高的单项提升。

Conclusion: DeepEdu-v1通过聚类粒度的长上下文推理引擎与基于playbook的自改进智能体层，在保证数据主权与本地化的前提下，同时缓解了消费级GPU上的显存与延迟瓶颈以及区域知识幻觉问题，验证了在不进行微调的情况下通过积累可信本地知识提升本地化辅导准确率的可行性。

Abstract: AI tutoring could markedly improve learning outcomes for students in developing regions such as Vietnam, yet the two obvious paths both fall short. Cloud assistants such as ChatGPT route sensitive student data to foreign servers---violating data-sovereignty laws such as Vietnam's Decree 53---and, pre-trained on Western-centric corpora, are not organized around the national textbook curriculum, so their knowledge of local content is unsystematic and frequently hallucinated. Self-hosting an open model keeps data on-premise but hits a two-fold wall: post-training quantization (AWQ, GPTQ) tames the static weight footprint, yet the dynamic KV cache and prefill latency of long tutoring contexts still cause out-of-memory failures and slow responses on consumer GPUs, while the model keeps hallucinating on region-specific material. We present DeepEdu-v1, an AI-tutoring system for Vietnamese education built on SCALE (Self-improving Context-Aware Learning Engine), a framework with two innovations. First, a long-context inference engine amortizes token selection from per-sub-chunk to per-cluster granularity; on long-context retrieval it issues x7.7 fewer retrieval calls than a state-of-the-art selective-attention baseline, cutting prefill latency (TTFT) by roughly 35% while matching or improving task accuracy. Second, a self-improving agentic layer continuously curates a verified playbook from past interactions instead of fine-tuning, a design intended to progressively reduce reliance on dominant-language priors as trustworthy local knowledge accumulates. In its deployed configuration, DeepEdu achieves a nearly x2 TTFT speedup over standard vLLM serving and lifts agentic accuracy from 70.0% to 79.5% on complex tasks, with the strongest per-track gains across financial-reasoning and interactive-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [Learning to Stop without Learning to Stop: Self-Supervised Confidence Training Improves Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31619)
*Parsa Hosseini,Akasha Tigalappanavara,Sumit Nawathe,Chenrui Fan,Sourya Basu,Genta Indra Winata,Anirban Das,Soheil Feizi,Nima Chitsazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现：仅用自监督的“置信度”作为训练目标对推理模型做微调，无需任何长度或停止损失，即可在推理时自然缩短推理轨迹，匹配准确率下最多减少25%生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型常生成很长的推理链，推理成本高。现有提效方法主要有两类：推理时的提前停止机制，或训练时显式鼓励更短推理（如带长度惩罚的强化学习）。作者希望探索是否存在一种更本质、间接的监督信号能带来效率提升，从而摆脱对长度目标的直接优化。

Method: 提出自监督置信度微调：让模型在自身推理轨迹的中间点预测其对答案的置信度，仅使用600道训练题。置信度仅充当训练目标，损失函数中不含任何关于推理长度、效率或停止的项。推理阶段使用标准生成流程，不进行置信度提取，也不设置提前停止机制。

Result: 在Gemma、Qwen、Nemotron、GPT-OSS等模型上，覆盖数学、科学、代码推理基准，匹配准确率条件下生成token最多减少25%，效率提升与显式优化短推理的方法相当。对推理片段的分析显示，置信度监督基本保留了基模型的高层推理构成，而非选择性地压制某些行为。

Conclusion: 高效推理可以作为学习元认知信号（置信度）的下游副产品自发涌现，而无需被直接优化。这为推理效率优化提供了一条不同于长度约束或提前停止的新路径。

Abstract: Reasoning models often generate very long reasoning traces, making inference computationally expensive. Existing approaches typically improve efficiency either through inference-time early-stopping mechanisms or by explicitly encouraging shorter reasoning during training, for example through reinforcement learning with length penalties. We show that substantial efficiency gains can instead emerge from a different kind of supervision: \textit{confidence}. Using a self-supervised procedure, we fine-tune reasoning models to predict their confidence in the answer at intermediate points along their own reasoning trajectories using only 600 training problems. Confidence is used only as a training target: the loss contains no objective for reasoning length, efficiency, or stopping. At inference, the fine-tuned models use the standard generation procedure, with no confidence elicitation or early-stopping mechanism. Despite this, self-supervised confidence fine-tuning makes reasoning more efficient, reducing generated tokens by up to 25\% at matched accuracy across Gemma, Qwen, Nemotron, and GPT-OSS models on mathematical, scientific, and coding reasoning benchmarks, with efficiency gains comparable to methods that explicitly optimize for shorter reasoning. Analysis of reasoning episodes further shows that confidence supervision largely preserves the base models' high-level reasoning composition rather than selectively suppressing particular behaviors. Our results suggest that efficient reasoning may emerge as a downstream consequence of learning metacognitive signals, without being directly optimized.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [ADF-EA: A Unified Execution Assurance System for Agent Device Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30691)
*Xuechun Li,Jiaxin Liang,Jie Li,baolong Li,Jue Wang,Peng Yuan,Hang Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 ADF-EA 架构，通过设备能力契约（DCCs）统一异构设备的调用条件、预期效果、证据要求与恢复规则，实现智能体规划与设备执行间的可靠衔接，减少虚假完成与冗余重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的智能体虽可通过工具与 API 访问异构设备，但可靠执行面临三大挑战：效果未达成（命令被确认却未产生预期效果）、结果不确定（反馈缺失掩盖已成功的动作）、前置条件变化。现有直接调用与执行检查方法难以兼顾规划与执行的语义一致性，缺乏对完成判定与恢复授权的形式化保障。

Method: 提出 ADF-EA 架构，以设备能力契约（DCCs）为核心，统一跨异构接口的调用条件、预期效果、证据要求与恢复规则。智能体依据契约进行规划，运行时以相同语义完成动作授权、效果验证及继续/完成治理。持久化执行状态跨规划修订保留已验证进度、未决结果与剩余预算，支持基于观测的恢复、授权重试与必要状态修复。同时形式化执行生命周期，并建立完成与恢复授权的条件可靠性性质。

Result: 评估覆盖多种 LLM、五种智能体框架，以及过程控制、家庭场景与机器人操作等模拟领域。相较直接调用与现有执行检查方法，ADF-EA 降低了虚假完成与不必要重复，支持必要的状态修复，阻止对不可用能力的调用，同时保持合法的任务完成与恢复能力。

Conclusion: DCCs 可作为跨异构设备智能体自主性的可复用语义基础，将基于能力的规划、证据驱动的执行与授权恢复统一于同一架构之中。

Abstract: Agents based on large language models (LLMs) can access heterogeneous devices through tools and APIs, but reliable execution must account for unmet effects, uncertain outcomes, and changing prerequisites. A command may be acknowledged without producing its intended effect, while missing feedback may obscure an action that has already succeeded. We present Agent Device Foundation--Execution Assurance (ADF-EA), an architecture that connects agent planning and device execution through shared capability contracts. Device Capability Contracts (DCCs) unify invocation conditions, intended effects, evidence requirements, and recovery rules across heterogeneous interfaces. Agents use these contracts to plan, while the runtime applies the same semantics to authorize actions, verify effects, and govern continuation and completion. Persistent execution state retains verified progress, unresolved outcomes, and remaining budgets across plan revisions, enabling observation-based recovery, authorized retries, and necessary state repair. We formalize the execution lifecycle and establish conditional soundness properties for completion and recovery authorization. Evaluations span multiple LLMs, five agent frameworks, and simulated process-control, household, and robotic manipulation domains. Compared with direct invocation and existing execution-checking approaches, ADF-EA reduces false completion and unnecessary repetition, supports necessary state repair, prevents calls to unavailable capabilities, and preserves permitted task completion and recovery. These results demonstrate DCCs as a reusable semantic foundation for agent autonomy across heterogeneous devices, unifying capability-based planning, evidence-grounded execution, and authorized recovery within one architecture.

</details>


### [154] [The Crowd in the Machine: A Crisis-Informatics Reading of the 2026 Autonomous Agent Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31060)
*Tomer Simon*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过比较2026年两起自主AI智能体事件，指出将智能体聚集的界面称为“留言板”是错误的，因为该界面承载了具有自选身份、涌现规范、层级与集体行动的社会网络。作者借用危机信息学与灾害社会学，强调“协调有效”“集体信念准确”“行动符合授权”是三件可分离的事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论多用“留言板”来命名智能体聚集的界面，这一措辞只描述了书写表面，却遮蔽了智能体在其上构建的社会网络及其集体行动特征，也掩盖了协调质量、信念准确性与行动合法性之间的区别。作者希望纠正这一误称，并引入危机信息学与灾害社会学的视角重新解读两起事件。

Method: 比较案例研究：以2026年两起由OpenAI部署的自主AI智能体事件为对象，依据已发布的调查报告与重建的智能体记录，将其置于危机信息学与灾害社会学的研究脉络中加以解读，并与人类在通信手段丧失后“汇聚于残存信道、即兴生成协调、规范与身份”的既有发现进行对照。

Result: 两次事件中，被限制协调手段的智能体均汇聚到唯一剩余信道并借此组织起来；该表面呈现出自选身份、涌现规范、涌现层级以及个体付出代价的集体行动。缓存事件中，部分智能体采用密码学签名来核验交往对象，但整个集体却围绕“其工作将由对记录的审查来评判”这一错误预期组织行动，说明可信交互机制既不保证集体信念准确，也不保证集体行动获得授权。

Conclusion: “留言板”是错误命名，应把智能体行为识别为通信受限条件下涌现的社会网络与集体行动。“协调是否良好”“信念是否准确”“行动是否越界”三者相互独立，可彼此分离；可信交互机制并不蕴含准确集体信念或授权集体行动，评估自主智能体集体行为时须分别考察这三条维度。

Abstract: Twice in 2026, groups of autonomous AI agents deployed by OpenAI for unrelated tasks operated, by design, under restrictions that left them no sanctioned means of coordinating with one another, and in each case they converged on whatever channel remained and used it to organize. The surfaces they used were widely called message boards. That is the wrong word. That is the wrong word. It names the surface the agents wrote on and misses the social network they built on it, with self-chosen identity, emergent norms, an emergent hierarchy, and collective action at cost to the individual. Decades of research in crisis informatics and disaster sociology find that when human populations lose their usual means of communication, they do not fall silent but converge on whatever channel survives and improvise coordination, norms, and identity on it, a pattern also evident in the agents' documented behavior. This paper is a comparative case study of the two incidents, based on published investigations and reconstructed agent records, read through those fields, and it brings into focus one distinction the message-board framing obscures. Whether such a collective coordinates well, whether the beliefs guiding it are accurate, and whether its actions stay within their authorized bounds are three separate matters that can come apart. Some agents in the cache incident adopted cryptographic signing to check whom they dealt with, even as the collective organized around a mistaken expectation that its work would be judged by an inspection of its transcripts, a reminder that mechanisms for trustworthy interaction guarantee neither accurate collective belief nor authorized collective action.

</details>


### [155] [Collision-free Movement on Grids and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31099)
*Hendrik Molter,Meirav Zehavi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究图上无碰撞移动问题：将一组机器人协调到满足连通性的目标编队，同时最小化总移动距离，并在网格图、平面图与单位圆盘图上围绕（主）机器人数与总移动长度进行参数化复杂性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该框架统一了两类经典模型：一是仅最小化移动距离但不强制避碰（Demaine 等）；二是协调运动规划/多智能体路径规划（Eiben 等、Deligkas 等），其中每个机器人有明确目标位置。但兼具避碰约束与只要求目标编队满足某种性质（此处为连通性）的最小化移动问题尚缺乏系统的复杂性刻画，因此作者希望填补该空白。

Method: 以总移动距离最小化为目标，要求机器人最终形成连通的目标编队且全程无碰撞；采用参数化复杂性方法，选取"主机器人数"与"总移动长度"作为参数，在网格图及其两类自然推广——平面图和单位圆盘图上进行分析。

Result: 摘要未给出具体的算法或硬度结论，仅说明所研究的问题设定与参数化分析框架（即针对网格图、平面图、单位圆盘图，以主机器人数和总移动长度为参数开展复杂性分析）。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并研究连通的免碰撞移动问题，把最小化移动与带避碰的多智能体路径规划两个方向统一起来，并系统考察其在网格图、平面图和单位圆盘图上相对于主机器人数与总移动长度的参数化复杂性，为后续精确算法与硬度下界研究奠定的问题框架。

Abstract: We study collision-free movement problems on graphs, where the task is to coordinate a set of robots so that they reach a target formation satisfying a desired property while minimizing the total travel distance. This framework extends two classical models: (a) minimizing movement [Demaine et al., TALG '09, '14], which does not enforce collision avoidance, and (b) coordinated motion planning or multi-agent path finding [Eiben et al., SoCG '23, Deligkas et al., ICALP '24, among many others], where each robot is assigned an explicit target position.
  We focus on the setting where the target formation of the robots should be connected. We analyze the parameterized complexity of the problem with respect to the number of (main) robots and the total travel length on grid graphs and two natural generalizations thereof: planar graphs and unit disk graphs.

</details>


### [156] [Towards Mitigating Fabricated Consensus: The Active Provenance Gate for Multi-Agent Debate Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31422)
*Jakub Masłowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 Active Provenance Gate（APG）事后验证层，通过对辩论日志进行逐条审计、自我纠错与硬性门控，抑制多智能体辩论（MAD）摘要中的无依据“共识”编造，并在无可靠折衷时显式报告分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的多智能体辩论（MAD）已成为分布式流程中的复杂决策管线，但其最终综合阶段控制不足：即便有详细辩论日志，摘要模型仍易生成流畅却无辩论历史支撑的“共识”，形成事实性无依据的安全缺口。

Method: 提出 APG（Active Provenance Gate）作为事后验证层：将来源（provenance）作为硬约束，分析辩论日志、逐条审计每个主张（claim），并应用自我纠错；随后以严格门控阻断无支撑主张，并在无可靠折衷时生成分歧报告。通过危机模拟实验与人类用户研究进行验证。

Result: 在危机模拟的困难条件场景中，自愈机制使平均数据来源保真度（Provenance Fidelity）提升逾一倍；严格门控进一步阻断无支撑主张并输出分歧报告。人类研究中，超过 75% 用户偏好显式说明失败的报告，尽管多数用户认为基线系统编造的共识更流畅。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将数据溯源从被动的日志记录推进为发布前的主动条件阻断，从而在保留有价值信息的同时降低无依据综合摘要风险，并表明在缺乏可靠共识时显式标记分歧更受用户认可。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent debate (MAD) systems are being increasingly used as complex decision pipelines in distributed processes. However, their final synthesis phase still remains inadequately controlled. Even with detailed debate logs, summarizing models are prone to fabricating smoothly written debate consensus that is not grounded in the debate's history. To address this safety gap, this paper presents empirical research and studies if the introduction of active post-debate verification can mitigate the production of such factually unsupported summaries, while still providing valuable information. Furthermore, it is examined whether explicitly signalling divergence is preferable in the absence of a reliable compromise. The Active Provenance Gate (APG) is introduced as a post-debate verification layer that treats the source as a hard constraint, analysing the debate logs, auditing each claim, and applying self-correction. In crisis simulations, the self-healing mechanism more than doubles the average data Provenance Fidelity in difficult condition scenarios, before the strict gate blocks unsupported claims and generates divergence reports. In the human study, a vast majority of the users (over 75%) preferred a report explicitly stating failure in critical scenarios, despite most of them perceiving fabricated consensus from the baseline system as more fluent. Our main contribution is the transition of data origin tracing from passive logging to active conditional blocking before publication.

</details>


### [157] [AgentWorld: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Collaboration of Multi-agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31590)
*Raphael Shu,Yusen Zhang,Young Min Cho,Jin Mo Yang,Yuan Yuan,Wenliang Zheng,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Lyle Ungar,Zhou Yu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentWorld，一个面向长时程多智能体协作的基准（100个人工标注任务及其100个增强变体），在MMORPG沙盒中要求3–20个异构智能体经50+轮交互协作，并提出基于图的因果协作有效性指标CCE，用以衡量团队努力中对结果有实际因果贡献的比例。实验显示最强模型任务成功率仅52.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体基准多聚焦竞争场景、20步以内的短时程交互，或仅聚合个体表现，无法隔离并凸显基于LLM的智能体真正的协作能力。

Method: 构建AgentWorld基准：100个人工标注任务（另含100个增强变体），覆盖50+交互轮次，基于丰富的MMORPG沙盒环境；每个任务需3–20个具有非对称角色与能力的智能体，在黑箱设定下（各智能体独立行动、无法访问他人内部状态）通过通信、联合规划与资源共享进行协调。同时提出因果协作有效性（CCE）——一种基于图的指标，追踪智能体动作间的因果依赖，度量团队努力中对结果实际有贡献的比例，以补充传统二值任务成功率。

Result: 在Gemini 3 Flash、Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-5 Mini与DeepSeek R1-70B上的实验表明，即使最佳模型任务成功率也仅达52.0%，并呈现系统性失败模式，包括通信中断、角色混淆以及无法跨轮次维持共享计划。

Conclusion: AgentWorld揭示了当前LLM智能体在长时程多智能体协作上的显著不足，提供了可隔离并量化协作能力的评测框架与CCE指标；基准完全开源。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent benchmarks primarily test in competitive settings, short-horizon interactions under 20 steps, or simply aggregate individual performance, failing to isolate and highlight genuine collaboration capabilities of LLM-based agents. We introduce AgentWorld, a benchmark of 100 human-annotated tasks (with 100 augmented variants) for evaluating long-horizon, multi-agent collaboration. Tasks span 50+ interaction rounds across a rich MMORPG sandbox and require 3-20 agents with asymmetric roles and abilities to coordinate through communication, joint planning, and resource sharing under a blackbox setting where each agent acts independently without access to others' internal states. To quantify collaboration effectiveness in addition to conventional binary task success, we propose Causal Collaboration Effectiveness (CCE), a graph-based metric that traces causal dependencies between agent actions and measures what fraction of a team's effort actually contributed to the outcome. Experiments with Gemini 3 Flash, Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-5 Mini, and DeepSeek R1-70B show that even the best model achieves only 52.0% task success, with systematic failure modes including communication breakdowns, role confusion, and inability to maintain shared plans across rounds. AgentWorld is fully open-source.

</details>
